With Create bins the folder becomes a bin named after it inside the target bin, each subfolder a sub-bin; without it every file goes into the target bin. | 33.24 | A folder dragged from the file manager onto the Media Pool (item area or a bin) is imported with its subfolders, as a bin named after it with sub-bins. Power bins take files only. | Fixed (new, importfolder; test_folderimport folderDroppedOnTheMediaPool; test_timelinedrop folderGoesToTheMediaPoolOnly) |

Numbered stills of one folder (name_0001.png ...) become one image sequence clip at the project frame rate, using the image sequence detection of the media engine (two or more numbered files). | Fixed (new, importfolder; test_folderscan hiddenJunkAndUnsupportedAreSkipped, numberedStillsBecomeOneSequence; test_folderimport imageSequenceIsOneClip) |
| 33.26 | Each folder is read in natural order: "clip2" before "clip10", case-insensitive, subfolders likewise. | Fixed (new, importfolder; test_folderscan naturalOrder, subfoldersOnAndOff; test_folderimport nestedFoldersCreateBinsInNaturalOrder) |
| 33.27 | Scanning and probing run on a worker thread behind a progress dialog with Cancel. Symlinked folders are followed, but a folder reached a second time (a symlink loop, or two links to it) is not entered again, so the scan always ends. | Decision (roll back rather than keep a partial import: the items are added in one step after the scan, so a cancel never leaves half a folder tree; test_folderscan cancel, symlinkLoopEnds; test_folderimport cancelImportsNothing, symlinkLoopDoesNotHang) |
| 33.28 | Files that fail to import are listed in one summary at the end (with the number of files already in the pool and of skipped files), never one dialog per file; no summary when everything new was imported. | Fixed (new, importfolder; test_folderimport nestedFoldersCreateBinsInNaturalOrder checks the failure list) |

| 33.31 | With the media pool focused, Delete / Backspace (also Shift+Backspace) delete the selected items (asking first when timeline clips use them, as the context menu's Delete) and Ctrl+A selects every item shown; in the bin list Delete / Backspace delete the current bin (Master and smart bins stay). | 33.37 | The media pool shows the same bin on every page (Media, Cut, Edit, Compositing): switching pages keeps the bin. After Import Media Folder, the bin made for the folder is shown, so the imported clips are in view. | Fixed (bughunt9 workflow: each page's media pool kept its own bin, File > Import always went to Master, and a folder import left the user looking at a bin without the new clips; `MainWindow::setCurrentPage()`, `ProjectController::shownMediaPool()`, `MediaPoolPanel::showImportedItems()`; bh9 spec33_37_importIntoTheShownBin). Only in project bins (Master and its bins), never in smart or power bins. | Fixed (poolbins, owner request: "make folders act like in DaVinci. Decision: in the list view a double-click opens, F2 renames. |

| 33.41 | Delete / Backspace on selected folders deletes their bins with the bin list's rules (asking first when they hold clips; one undo step "Delete Bin"). The folder context menu offers Open, Rename Bin, Delete Bin, New Bin, Import Media Folder… and New Smart Bin…. | Fixed (pf renameAndDeleteWithUndo, folderContextMenu) |
| 33.42 | With a search, the clips of the bin and its sub-bins are searched as before; folders whose names do not match are hidden. A clip colour filter shows clips only. | Fixed (pf searchHidesFolders) |
| 33.43 | Sort by Type (the sort menu; the list view's Type column, hidden by default, also on a header click): timelines, video, audio, stills, compound clips, multicam clips, subtitles, then by name. Folders always come first, whatever the sort (Import Order too); Descending reverses the order among the folders and among the clips but keeps the folders first. | Fixed (pf sortByType) |
| 33.44 | Preferences > Editing > "Import new media into": Current bin (default, as in Resolve) or Master bin. It applies to every import without a bin of its own: File > Import (Ctrl+I), Import Media Folder and Subtitle, the media pool's Import button, Media page > Media Storage > Add to Media Pool (files and folders, which used to go into Master), drops from the file manager onto the clip area and media imported by a timeline drop. A drop onto a bin of the bin list or onto a folder still goes into that bin. All of them ask `importTargetBin()` (ui/common/MediaImport.h; `importMediaWithFeedback()` and `importMediaFoldersWithFeedback()` use it when no bin is given), except files dropped on the timeline (5.6), which ask `TimelineController::targetBin()`: the same rule over the Edit page media pool's current bin. | Fixed (poolbins, owner request; `prefs::importIntoMasterBin()`, key `mediaPool/importTarget`; pf importTarget, dropOnFolderMoves; test_viewmenu ctrlIImportFollowsThePreference, mediaStorageAddFollowsThePreference; test_timelinedrop spec33_44_timelineDropFollowsTheImportPreference) |
| 33.45 | The Metadata panel's Clip Details values (Name, File, Folder, codecs, ...) are readable in every widget style: the value column takes the panel's free width, clipped with the whole value in the tooltip. | Fixed (README screenshots: with Breeze the values were 1 px wide, Fusion hid it; `MetadataPanel` sets `AllNonFixedFieldsGrow` on both forms) |
| 33.46 | The mouse's Back and Forward side buttons, over the clip area (thumbnails, list, strips) or the bin list, go back to the bin opened before and forward again, like a file manager. Opening a bin (double-clicking a folder, clicking a bin in the bin list, Find in Media Pool, Master) adds it to the panel's history and drops the bins ahead of it; Back and Forward themselves do not. The side buttons never change the selection. | Fixed (poolnav, owner request: "it feels a bit clunky"; pn = tests/panels/test_poolnavigation.cpp; pn backAndForwardButtons). Decision: no Back / Forward buttons in the toolbar, and no Alt+Left / Alt+Right keys: the Kdenlive and Premiere Pro keymap presets use them for edit points and trim nudges. Each page's media pool keeps its own history. |
| 33.47 | Bins deleted since are skipped by Back and Forward (and come back when an undo restores them). Deleting the shown bin falls back to Master without an entry of its own. | Fixed (pn historySkipsDeletedBins) |
| 33.48 | The thumbnail size of the clip area's grid: a slider in the media pool toolbar (shown with the thumbnail view) and Ctrl+wheel over the thumbnails, one step per notch (half notches of high-resolution wheels add up), from 64 to 240 px wide posters in nine steps (144, the old fixed size, is the default). Remembered in the settings (`mediaPool/thumbnailWidth`), shared by the pages' media pools. Small thumbnails leave out the duration badge and the "Linked" badge when they no longer fit; the name stays, placeholder glyphs shrink. The list and strip views keep their sizes. | Fixed (pn ctrlWheelZoomsThumbnails). Decision: posters are decoded at least at the default height, so zooming out needs no new decode; larger sizes request larger posters. |
| 33.49 | One mouse wheel notch scrolls the thumbnail grid and the strip view by a third of a row (it used to jump three whole rows); touchpads scroll by the pixels they report. Ctrl+wheel zooms (33.48); Shift / Alt + wheel keep Qt's page and horizontal scrolling. The list view, the bin list and the Edit page's Effects library scroll by short rows already and are unchanged. | Fixed (pn wheelScrollsLessThanARow; `MediaPoolPanel::scrollByWheel()`) |
| 33.50 | The Media Pool header never squeezes the search box: when the panel is narrow the thumbnail size slider shrinks first (70 px down to 30 px) and then hides, so the search placeholder always shows in full; the search box has no minimum width of its own. Ctrl+wheel zoom works with the slider hidden. | Fixed (searchBoxStaysReadableBesideTheSlider) |

## 35. Cut page (cutpage branch, roadmap v2.5)

Fast single-timeline editing on its own page (Shift+3), sharing the project, the active
timeline, the selection, the undo stack and the timeline controller (tool, snapping, targets,
clipboard) with the Edit page. Written from general knowledge of how Resolve's Cut page works.

Tests: cp = tests/app/test_cutpage.cpp, ce = tests/core/test_cutedits.cpp, st =
tests/core/test_sourcetape.cpp.

| # | Expected behaviour | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 35.1 | The page bar's Cut button (between Media and Edit) and Shift+3 open the Cut page; the interface bar offers the media pool toggle there. | Fixed (new; cp pageIsEnabledAndSwitchesWithShift3) |
| 35.2 | Layout: media pool on the left, one large viewer on the right, the tool bar of smart edits below them, then the dual timeline. Switching between Cut and Edit keeps the playhead, the selection, the clips and the undo history (one project, one active timeline). | Fixed (new; cp sharesTimelinePlayheadAndSelectionWithEditPage) |
| 35.3 | Dual timeline: the upper timeline always shows the whole timeline fitted to the width, with the playhead and a band marking what the lower timeline shows; the lower one is zoomed (about 20 s across) with the playhead held in its centre, so it scrolls under the playhead during playback and navigation. Clicking or dragging in the upper one moves the playhead; scrolling the lower one by hand (wheel) moves the playhead too. Both switch the viewer to Timeline. | Fixed (new; cp dualTimelineFollowsThePlayhead) |
| 35.4 | Viewer modes Source Clip / Source Tape / Timeline (buttons in the viewer header; Q switches between the last source mode and Timeline). A click on a media pool clip opens it in Source Clip mode, or, in Source Tape mode, moves the tape to that clip. Each mode keeps its own clip, position and marks. | Fixed (new; cp viewerModesAndQ) |
| 35.5 | Source Tape: every clip of the current bin, in the media pool's view order (sort, search and filter apply), played back to back as one tape, with a bar of clip boundaries under the viewer (click to jump). In / out marks work across clips: a marked range is split at the clip boundaries into one source range per clip, and the edit buttons place them back to back. Only an in mark: to the end of that clip; only an out mark: from the start of its clip; no marks: the whole clip under the tape position. Timelines, compound / multicam clips and subtitles are not on the tape; stills get the still length. | Fixed (new; st, cp sourceTapeMarksAcrossClipBoundaries) |
| 35.6 | Smart Insert inserts the source at the edit point nearest to the playhead (clip starts and ends on any track, frame 0 or the timeline end; ties go to the earlier point), so a clip is never split; later clips on the auto-select tracks ripple. The playhead goes to the end of the new clip. While the Cut page is shown, F9 runs Smart Insert. | Fixed (new; ce smartInsert*, cp smartInsertPicksNearestEditPointInOneUndoStep, insertKeyRunsSmartInsertOnTheCutPage) |
| 35.7 | Append puts the source after the last clip of the timeline; nothing moves (Shift+F12 on the Cut page). | Fixed (new; ce appendGoesAfterTheLastClip) |
| 35.8 | Ripple Overwrite replaces the clip under the playhead (destination video track first) with the source at its own length; everything after it ripples by the difference (Shift+F10 on the Cut page). Without a clip under the playhead nothing happens and the tool bar says why. | Fixed (new; ce rippleOverwrite*, cp refusedEditLeavesNoUndoStep) |
| 35.9 | Place On Top puts the source at the playhead on the first track above the destination that is free for the whole source (a new track if none is); all clips of one edit go on that one track (F12 on the Cut page). | Fixed (new; ce placeOnTopUsesFirstFreeTrackForAllSegments) |
| 35.10 | Close Up places the source's picture on top at the playhead, zoomed to 150 % around the centre (a cutaway to a closer shot; the sound below continues). | Decision: Resolve detects faces to frame the close up; Kinoklip zooms on the centre (`cutedits::kCloseUpZoom`). Video only. (ce closeUpIsZoomedVideoOnTop) |
| 35.11 | Source Overwrite places the source's picture on the track above so that it lines up by timecode with the clip under the playhead: the source frame with the same timecode as the timeline frame under the playhead lands on the playhead. Timecode = Clip Attributes start timecode, else the file's embedded one. No clip under the playhead, a missing timecode or a marked range that does not contain that moment: refused with a message. | Decision (simplified): only the first source range decides the alignment; video only; no multi-angle search. (ce sourceOverwrite*) |
| 35.12 | Every smart edit is one undo step named after the button; the new clips are selected (video and audio linked per source clip) and the playhead moves to their end. A refused edit leaves no undo step. | Fixed (new; cp smartInsertPicksNearestEditPointInOneUndoStep, appendPlaceOnTopCloseUpRippleOverwrite, refusedEditLeavesNoUndoStep) |
| 35.13 | Smart edit material: Source Clip mode the marked range of the clip (whole clip unmarked); Source Tape mode as 35.5; Timeline mode the clips selected in the media pool, whole, in view order. Destination tracks are the Edit page's (Alt+n / Ctrl+Alt+n). F10 / F11 / Shift+F11 on the Cut page do the Edit page's overwrite / replace / fit to fill with the Cut viewer's source. | Fixed (new; cp insertKeyRunsSmartInsertOnTheCutPage) |
| 35.14 | Lower timeline editing: select, move and trim as on the Edit page with the selection tool, except that dragging a clip edge is a ripple trim (later clips follow, no gap) - the Cut page's default; the narrow centre of a cut between two clips rolls. Dragging clips past the outermost track creates a new track (21.8) and a double-click on a compound clip opens it at the frame under the pointer (26.14), as on the Edit page (the lower timeline is the same widget). Blade at the playhead (Ctrl+\ as in the keymap), Delete / Backspace and ripple delete, Ctrl+T and the tool bar's dissolve button for a cross dissolve at the nearest edit. | Fixed (new; cp edgeDragRipplesInTheLowerTimeline, movePastTheTopTrackCreatesATrack, bladeAndDissolveFromTheToolBar) |
| 35.15 | Audio tracks are compact in the lower timeline (about half height); the upper timeline gives audio about a third of its height. | Fixed (new; `TimelineViewOptions::audioHeightScale`) |
| 35.16 | Media pool: thumbnail, list and strip views (strip: a film strip of thumbnails across each clip, Cut page only), with the media pool's sort menu (import order, name, duration, resolution, frame rate, codec, clip colour, usage, dates; ascending / descending). | Fixed (new; cp stripViewOnlyOnTheCutPage) |
| 35.17 | Fast Review (tool bar button) plays the timeline faster: each shot (the stretch between two edit points) plays at its length / 2 s, at least real time and at most 8x, so long shots go by quickly. Any transport command ends it. | Decision (simplified): no audio pitch handling at the higher rates. (ce fastReviewRateFollowsClipLength, cp fastReviewSpeedGrowsWithShotLength) |
| 35.18 | The cut.* commands are enabled only while the Cut page is shown; the Edit page's F9 / F12 / Shift+F10 / Shift+F12 keep their Edit page meaning there. | Fixed (new; cp cutActionsOnlyOnTheCutPage) |
| 35.19 | Sync bin (the bin of clips in sync with the timeline clip under the playhead). | Deferred: needs per-clip sync data across cameras. |
| 35.20 | Boring detector (marks long shots and jump cuts). | Deferred. |
| 35.21 | Trim editor window (the full-screen trim view with both sides of the cut). | Deferred: needs a second render path in the viewer (as 8.15). |
| 35.22 | Transitions other than a cross dissolve from the Cut page tool bar (wipes, smooth cut). | Deferred: the Effects library on the Edit page has the other transitions. |
| 35.23 | Audio trim view (waveforms enlarged while trimming). | Deferred. |
| 35.24 | Camera-angle features (multicam source tape, sync by angle, Source Overwrite searching every angle). | Deferred: multicam editing is on the Edit page. |
| 35.25 | Inspector, effects and titles panels on the Cut page. | Deferred: use the Edit page (the selection carries over). |
| 35.26 | Dragging media onto the Cut page's timeline viewer shows the smart edits as an overlay (Smart Insert, Append, Ripple Overwrite, Close Up, Place On Top, Source Overwrite, with their keys); dropping on one runs it with the dropped clips (one undo step, named after the edit). | Fixed (polish round 2; cp dropOnTheViewerOffersTheSmartEdits). Decision: only the timeline viewer takes the drop (as on the Edit page); the source modes keep their own drag. |
| 35.27 | A Cut page edit selects the new clip with its video part current, so the Edit page Inspector stays on the Video tab (as 24.15). | Fixed (bughunt2: CutEditor selected the audio part last and the Inspector switched to Audio after every Cut page edit; test_bughunt2 spec35_27_cutEditKeepsVideoTab) |
| 35.28 | A marked source range edited into a timeline of another frame rate (Cut page Append and the other smart edits, Edit page insert / overwrite, drops) covers the whole range: every timeline frame that starts inside it, so the Out frame is shown. In 1:00 / Out 7:00 of a 25 fps source (151 frames, 6.04 s) is a 6:01 clip (145 frames) on a 24 fps timeline. Never longer than the media; same-rate ranges are exact. | Fixed (bughunt10: rounding bug, the frames were rounded down to 6:00 and the Out frame was lost; `TimelineController::makeMediaClips()`, `usToFramesCeil()`; test_bughunt10 spec35_28_appendOfAnotherRateCoversTheMarkedRange) |

## 36. Animations (v2.2)

Small tweened animations of the timeline views (Edit page, both Cut page timelines, stacked
timelines), added on the owner's request so edits read more clearly. They are display only:
the timeline data and the undo stack change at once, exactly as without them. Numbers 36 and 37
are left for the Color and Audio pages.

Tests: an = tests/timeline/test_timelineanimation.cpp.

| # | Expected behaviour | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 36.1 | Preferences > UI Settings > "Animate timeline edits" (default on) and "Animation speed" (0.5x - 2x, default 1x) are stored and apply at once to every open timeline; turning them off mid-animation shows the final state immediately. KDE's global animation speed (System Settings, kdeglobals AnimationDurationFactor) scales the durations and its "instant" setting turns them off. | Fixed (new; an preferencePersistsAndAppliesLive) |
| 36.2 | With animations off the view always shows the model; nothing extra runs (no snapshot, no timer). Offscreen (the tests) they default to off unless KK_ANIMATIONS is set; KK_NO_ANIMATIONS turns them off anywhere. | Fixed (new; an disabledShowsTheModelAtOnce) |
| 36.3 | After a committed edit, every clip whose start, length or track changed slides from where it was drawn to its new place (OutCubic, about 180 ms): ripple delete and close gap (later clips slide left), insert / overwrite / append and the Cut page's smart edits (later clips slide right), keyboard trims (Shift+[ / ], Ctrl+Shift+[ / ], edit point trims), nudges (, and .), track changes and undo / redo. The tween always ends exactly on the model. | Fixed (new; an rippleDeleteSlidesAndLandsOnTheModel, newEditRestartsFromTheDisplayedState) |
| 36.4 | New clips (inserts, drops from the media pool or a file manager, paste) grow in from about 70 % of their height and fade in (light OutBack, about 160 ms, no visible bounce); removed clips fade out and shrink where they were drawn. Undoing a delete slides the clip back from its fading ghost. | Fixed (new; an insertedClipAppears, rippleDeleteSlidesAndLandsOnTheModel) |
| 36.5 | Blade (the blade tool, Ctrl+\ split at the playhead, the Cut page button): a bright line wipes down the cut and fades (about 220 ms) while the two halves are drawn a hair (4 px) apart and settle together. Undoing a cut lets the left half grow back without a ghost. | Fixed (new; an bladeFlashesAndSeparatesTheHalves) |
| 36.6 | Mouse trims, ripple trims and rolls follow the pointer 1:1 while dragging (no animation during any open edit, in this view or another view of the same timeline); on release the trimmed edges flash once. The selected edit point's highlight pulses once when it is selected and after a keyboard trim moves it. | Fixed (new; an liveDragsAreNotAnimated) |
| 36.7 | A new edit during an animation restarts from what is drawn at that moment, never from the old model state, so nothing jumps. | Fixed (new; an newEditRestartsFromTheDisplayedState) |
| 36.8 | Hit testing, snapping and every command use the model positions, also mid-animation. | Fixed (new; an hitTestsUseTheModelMidAnimation) |
| 36.9 | The snap indicator fades in and out (about 120 ms). | Fixed (new; an snapIndicatorFades) |
| 36.10 | Isolated playhead jumps (Up / Down to edit points, Home / End, markers, a click in the Cut page's upper timeline) glide the drawn playhead line (about 140 ms); the playhead value changes at once. Playback, scrubbing, the Cut page's centred lower timeline and changes less than 100 ms apart (drags, key auto-repeat) are not animated. | Fixed (new; an playheadJumpGlides) |
| 36.11 | Performance: only the timeline viewport repaints during an animation, at the display refresh rate; a change on a timeline with more than 50000 clips, or one that adds / removes more than 200 clips (a load), is not animated. | Fixed (new; test_timelinebench, test_resizebench unchanged) |
| 36.12 | Track height tweens, zoom smoothing, page switch cross-fades and Inspector section animations. | Deferred: clips already tween vertically when tracks are added or removed; the rest was left out to keep page switching and zoom immediate. |
| 36.13 | Menus, context menus, submenus, combo-box lists, completer lists, the Compositing page's node picker (Shift+Space) and tooltips open and close at once, with no fade, roll or blink of the chosen item, whatever the desktop's animation settings (Qt's UI_AnimateMenu / UI_FadeMenu / UI_AnimateCombo / UI_*Tooltip effects, which Windows and KDE turn on from their settings, are switched off; the style hints SH_Menu_FadeOutOnHide and SH_Menu_FlashTriggeredItem return 0). The timeline animations of this section are not affected. | Fixed (redditfb, Reddit feedback on 1.0.0-beta.1: fading menus slowed the work; `instantpopups::install()` from `theme::apply()`, `MenuMetricsStyle::styleHint()`; tests/app/test_theme.cpp spec36_13_popupsAppearInstantly) |
| 36.14 | Compositor fades of popups: every popup window is marked so the compositor does not animate it where a window can opt out: on X11 `_KDE_NET_WM_SKIP_CLOSE_ANIMATION` (KWin's "Fading popups" effect skips the closing fade; its opening fade has no opt-out), on Windows `DWMWA_TRANSITIONS_FORCEDISABLED`. On Wayland no protocol allows it: with KWin's "Fading popups" on (System Settings > Desktop Effects) menus still fade there, as in every other application. | Partly (redditfb; `instantpopups::markWindow()`; X11 and Windows hints not verified on a real KWin / Windows desktop; the marking itself is covered by spec36_13) |

## 37. Audio page (audiopage branch, roadmap v2.5)

Mixing on its own page (Shift+7): the mixer with buses, EQ and dynamics per track and bus,
fader / pan automation, peak meters and loudness metering. Written from general knowledge of how
Resolve's audio page works and from standard DSP (RBJ cookbook biquads, ITU-R BS.1770-4 /
EBU R128); our own names, layout drawing and icons. It shares the project, the active timeline,
the playhead, the selection, the undo stack and the timeline controller with the Edit and Cut
pages.

Tests: ap = tests/app/test_audiopage.cpp, mg = tests/render/test_mixergraph.cpp, dsp =
tests/render/test_mixerdsp.cpp, md = tests/core/test_mixerdata.cpp.

| # | Expected behaviour | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 37.1 | The page bar's Audio button (between Color and Deliver) and Shift+7 open the Audio page; the interface bar offers the Mixer toggle there. | Fixed (new; ap pageIsEnabledAndSwitchesWithShift7) |
| 37.2 | Layout: a small viewer at the top left (Audio > Float Viewer puts it in its own small window and back), the monitoring panel (meters, loudness) across the top, the timeline below it with the audio tracks twice as tall (waveforms, level lines and fade handles of section 31) and the video tracks collapsed to the smallest row, the large mixer on the right. Switching pages keeps the playhead, the selection, the clips and the undo history; the timeline is edited with the Edit page's tools. | Fixed (new; ap sharesTimelinePlayheadAndUndoWithEditPage, timelineShowsTallAudioAndCollapsedVideo, pageActionsOnlyOnTheAudioPage) |
| 37.3 | Audio track headers on the Audio page: R (record arm), S, M, lock and auto select buttons, the channel format (Mono / Stereo) on the second line and a level meter at the header's right edge. The header's mute and solo are the mixer's (one state). | Fixed (new; ap timelineShowsTallAudioAndCollapsedVideo). Decision: the arm button is shown disabled (recording is deferred, 37.24). Changed by 75.1: the arm button works and the Edit page has it too. |
| 37.4 | Mixer strips, one per audio track and then one per bus after a separator. Track strip, top to bottom: input (the track), EQ and Dyn buttons (lit when on; a click opens the small editor window), bus assignment (a menu of checkable buses), pan knob, automation mode (Off / Read / Touch / Write), R / M / S, the fader with a dB scale (+12 ... -inf, 0 dB at about three quarters; drag, Shift for fine steps, double-click 0 dB, wheel 0.5 dB) next to its meter, the value, the name with the channel format (right-click: Mono / Stereo). Bus strip: Sum, EQ, Dyn, Output, M, fader and meter, name and format (right-click: Mono / Stereo, Rename, Delete). The Edit page's Mixer panel shows the same strips. | Fixed (new; ap panMuteSoloAndBusFaderSteps, sharesTimelinePlayheadAndUndoWithEditPage) |
| 37.5 | Buses: Bus 1 (the main bus, stereo) always exists; Audio > Add Bus (also in the mixer's title bar) adds a stereo bus, a bus strip's menu makes it mono, renames or deletes it (not Bus 1). Every track feeds Bus 1 by default and can feed any number of buses (none = not heard). The Output button picks the bus that playback plays and export renders (default Bus 1); deleting it makes Bus 1 the output again, and tracks stop feeding a deleted bus. | Fixed (new; mg busRoutingSummingAndOutputBus, md busOps, ap busesAndRouting) |
| 37.6 | Signal flow of a track: its clips (clip volume and level line, clip pan, fades and crossfades, section 31) are summed, then channel format (mono = (L + R) / 2 on both sides) -> EQ -> compressor -> limiter -> pan -> fader -> mute / solo -> the assigned buses. A bus: sum -> channel format -> EQ -> compressor -> limiter -> fader -> mute; the output bus goes through the master soft clipper to playback and export. Pan law: constant power, -3 dB (a centred signal is 3.01 dB below a hard-panned one; normalised to unity at the centre, so an untouched track plays exactly as before the mixer existed); stereo tracks pan as a balance. | Fixed (new; mg neutralMixerPlaysTheSourceUnchanged, panLawIsMinus3dBConstantPower, eqAndDynamicsInTheMix) |
| 37.7 | EQ: 6 bands per track and bus. Defaults: band 1 high-pass 80 Hz and band 6 low-pass 16 kHz (both off), band 2 low shelf 100 Hz, bands 3 and 4 bells at 500 Hz and 2 kHz, band 5 high shelf 8 kHz (0 dB: neutral). Each band can be any of high pass, low shelf, bell, notch, high shelf, low pass (RBJ cookbook biquads, 12 dB per octave filters), 20 Hz - 20 kHz, +-24 dB, Q 0.1 - 18. The EQ window draws the exact response of the mixer's own filters with a numbered handle per band: dragging moves frequency and gain (filters and the notch only in frequency) and switches the EQ on, the wheel changes Q; below it a row per band (on, type, frequency, gain, Q). Parameter changes glide over about 10 ms (no zipper noise); switching a band or the EQ crossfades. | Fixed (new; dsp bellBoostsItsCentreFrequency, cutAndShelves, filtersAndNotch, neutralAndDisabledEqChangeNothing, eqChangesGlideWithoutJumps; ap eqHandleDragIsOneUndoStep) |
| 37.8 | Dynamics per track and bus: compressor (threshold, ratio, attack, release, soft knee, make-up), stereo linked, feed-forward on the sample peak; attack and release are time constants of the gain reduction in dB (after a level step the reduction reaches 63 % of its final value in one attack time). Limiter: a ceiling no sample exceeds (instant attack) and a release. The window shows the transfer curve and the current gain reduction. | Fixed (new; dsp compressorStaticCurve, compressorReducesAndTimesItsEnvelope, makeupGainAndLimiterCeiling) |
| 37.9 | Solo and mute: with any track soloed only the soloed tracks are heard; a muted track is silent even when soloed; bus mute silences the bus. Mute, solo, fader and pan changes glide over about 5 ms; a muted track is not decoded once its gain reached zero. | Fixed (new; mg soloAndMute; dsp channelProcessorGainsGlide) |
| 37.10 | Channel format: a mono track or bus folds its input to (L + R) / 2 on both sides before its processing; stereo keeps both sides. | Fixed (new; mg monoTrackFoldsTheChannels) |
| 37.11 | Track automation of the fader and pan: Read (default) plays the recorded curve, Off plays the static fader, Touch records while the control is held during playback (from press to release; holding it still keeps writing as the playhead moves; keys passed over are replaced), Write records the whole play pass whether the control is touched or not. One recording pass is one undo step. Stopped, a control moved on a track whose curve plays sets the key at the playhead. The curves use the existing keyframe system (Bezier keys, timeline frames). | Fixed (new; mg faderAndPanAutomation; ap touchAutomationRecordsWhilePlaying, writeModeRecordsTheWholePass, stoppedFaderMoveSetsTheKeyAtThePlayhead; md automationValuesAndModes). Decision (simplified): Write records fader and pan of every Write track, Touch returns to reading at once on release (no glide back), Latch / Trim modes are not offered; track automation is anchored to the timeline and does not move with ripple edits (the clip level line, section 31, moves with its clip). |
| 37.12 | Every mixer gesture is one undo step named after it: a fader or pan drag, a click on a button, a menu choice, an EQ handle drag, a committed field value. | Fixed (new; ap faderDragIsOneUndoStep, panMuteSoloAndBusFaderSteps, busesAndRouting, eqHandleDragIsOneUndoStep; md mixerEditsAreOneUndoStep) |
| 37.13 | Meters: post-fader sample peaks per track (mixer strip and timeline header), per bus and of the output, with a peak hold mark (1.5 s, then falling) and a bar falling back at 24 dB/s. Colour zones from the colour scheme: positive below -18 dBFS, neutral up to -6 dBFS, negative above. They show the audio being heard (the readings are time-stamped by the mixer, which runs ahead of the device) and update at most about 30 times a second. | Fixed (new; ap metersFeedTheWidgets; mg metersReportTracksBusesAndOutput) |
| 37.14 | Loudness on the output bus (ITU-R BS.1770-4 / EBU R128): momentary (400 ms), short-term (3 s) and integrated (gated: -70 LUFS absolute, -10 LU relative) LUFS, the true peak maximum (4x oversampled) in dBTP, against the -23 LUFS target; Reset (Audio > Reset Loudness Meter) restarts the integrated value and the maximum. A 1 kHz stereo sine at -23 dBFS reads -23.0 LUFS. | Fixed (new; dsp loudnessOfEbuSine, loudnessGating, truePeakFindsInterSamplePeaks; ap metersFeedTheWidgets) |
| 37.15 | Playback and export mix identically (one AudioMixer, the output bus): a range mixes to the same samples in one call, in playback chunks and after a seek (filter memory is cleared when a mix does not continue the previous one). The mixer is real-time safe in steady state (no allocations; buffers and channel state only grow when a track or bus appears). Sound-only edits (mixer, automation, clip volume) do not re-render or drop pictures while playing. | Fixed (new; mg rangesMixTheSameInChunksAndAfterSeeks) |
| 37.16 | Render cache and mixer settings: changing the mix never plays stale audio. | Decision: the render cache stores pictures only (section 30); audio is always mixed live from the current timeline, so there is nothing to invalidate. |
| 37.17 | Project format 12 stores the mixer ("mix" on audio tracks, "mixer" on timelines; only non-default values); versions 1 - 11 load unchanged with a neutral mixer (Bus 1 only, every track routed to it, 0 dB); a damaged mixer is repaired on load (missing Bus 1 added, duplicate ids dropped, unknown routes removed, values clamped). | Fixed (new; md jsonRoundTrip, defaultMixerWritesNothing, olderFormatsLoadWithANeutralMixer, damagedMixerDataIsRepaired) |
| 37.18 | Everything the page paints (meters, faders, knobs, EQ curve, transfer curve, header meters) uses the colour scheme (`kk::schemeColors()`). | Fixed (new) |
| 37.19 | `--screenshot audio[:eq\|dynamics]`: the Audio page with a demo mix and meter readings (KK_SCREENSHOT_IMPORT imports media first); the editor window is composited into the grab. | Fixed (new) |
| 37.20 | Gate / expander in the dynamics section. | Deferred (optional at this level). |
| 37.21 | Loudness range (LRA), loudness history graph, loudness normalisation on export. | Deferred. |
| 37.22 | Sends with their own level and pre / post fader, bus pan, bus-to-bus routing, automation of EQ / dynamics parameters and of bus faders, automation curves drawn and edited on the timeline. | Deferred: routes are unity post-fader sums; automation is edited with the fader and pan. |
| 37.23 | Audio scrubbing (hearing the audio while dragging the playhead). | Fixed by the editpolish branch (spec 23.7, Shift+S; snippets go through the same mixer, so EQ, dynamics and bus routing are heard while scrubbing). |
| 37.24 | Recording and ADR (beyond the disabled arm button). | Voice-over recording is done (75); ADR cue lists stay deferred. |
| 37.25 | VST / LV2 plugins. | Deferred. |
| 37.26 | 5.1, 7.1 and immersive (object-based) formats; tracks and buses are mono or stereo. | Deferred. |
| 37.27 | A sound library (searchable effects library with preview). | Deferred. |
| 37.28 | Dialogue leveler. | Deferred. |
| 37.29 | Elastic wave (warping audio in time inside a clip). | Deferred. |
| 37.30 | Hardware audio console / control surface mapping. | Deferred. |
| 37.31 | VCA groups. | Deferred. |
| 37.32 | Flex bus details (bus-to-bus feeds, bus formats beyond mono / stereo, bus groups). | Deferred. |
| 37.33 | Effects beyond EQ and dynamics (reverb, delay, de-esser, noise reduction, pitch, ...). | Deferred. |
| 37.34 | Audio page track headers taller than two button rows keep the track name on the first line up to the meter and put the buttons and the channel format on the second line (with the arm button and meter on one line the name was cut to "A..."). | Fixed (polish round 2; ap timelineShowsTallAudioAndCollapsedVideo) |
| 37.35 | Clip > Normalize Audio Levels (also in the clip context menu): Normalization Mode Sample Peak Program (dBFS), True Peak (dBTP) or ITU-R BS.1770-4 (LUFS), a Target Level (defaults -9 dBFS, -2 dBTP, -23 LUFS) and, for several clips, Set Level Relative (one common gain: the loudest clip reaches the target) or Independent (each clip reaches it). The clips' source audio is measured off the GUI thread with a progress bar; Cancel stops it and changes nothing. Then the clip volumes are set in one undo step "Normalize Audio Levels" (keyframed volumes move by the same change). Targets: the selected audio clips and the audio of selected video clips. | Fixed (polish round 4; test_audionormalize). Decision: Independent is the default (several clips to the same level); the measurement is the source audio before the clip volume, so normalizing twice gives the same result. |
| 37.36 | The Audio page's timeline starts at the top: a thin strip of collapsed video tracks, then the audio tracks, with no empty band above them (the Edit page keeps the V/A divider near the middle). | Fixed (bughunt7: the divider was centred as on the Edit page, leaving a large empty band above the collapsed video tracks; test_bughunt7 spec37_36_audioPageTracksStartAtTheTop) |

## 38. Project Manager (projmgr branch, roadmap v4 part 1)

Where a Resolve user starts: a window listing the projects of a project library, to create,
open and organise them, shown at start-up and from File > Project Manager. Written from general
knowledge of how such a project manager behaves; our own layout drawing, names and icons. The
library logic is in `src/projects`, the window in `src/ui/projects`.

Tests: pl = tests/projects/test_projectlibrary.cpp, pm = tests/app/test_projectmanager.cpp.

| # | Expected behaviour | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 38.1 | A project library: one folder holding the projects, default `~/Videos/Kinoklip Projects` (created when missing), changeable in Preferences > Project Save and Load ("Project library location", Default button). Its sub-folders are the library's folders; a project is a file of a known kind (`.kkproj`, `.kinoklip`); other files are not listed. | Fixed (new; pl defaultFolderIsVideosKinoklipProjects, listsFoldersAndProjectsOnly) |
| 38.2 | File > Project Manager and Shift+1 show the Project Manager over the editor (window-modal). Shift+1 no longer opens the file dialog; File > Open Project (Ctrl+O) still does. | Fixed (new; pm fileMenuAndSwitchingProjects) |
| 38.3 | Start-up: crash recovery first; then a project given on the command line opens directly; otherwise the Project Manager is shown, or, with Preferences > "At start-up: Reopen the last project", the most recent project that still exists. `--screenshot`, `--render` and offscreen runs never show it. | Fixed (new; pm fileMenuAndSwitchingProjects for the reopen; the start-up order is in main.cpp) |
| 38.4 | Thumbnail cards: the poster frame of the project's active timeline (the frame under its saved playhead, else its first video clip), name, last modified, and "duration · resolution · frame rate"; a list view with sortable columns Name, Date Modified, Duration, Resolution, Frame Rate, Kind, Location. Switching views keeps the selection. | Fixed (new; pm cardsListSearchAndSort, detailsAndBrokenCards; pl missingMediaAndPosterSource) |
| 38.5 | Search filters the shown folder by name (case-insensitive); sort by Name, Date Modified or Duration, ascending or descending (default: newest first); folders always come first; the card size slider zooms the cards. View, sort and size are remembered. | Fixed (new; pm cardsListSearchAndSort) |
| 38.6 | Folders: New Folder, double-click (or Enter) enters a folder, the path above the cards goes back (each part is a button), Backspace goes up. Projects and folders are dragged onto a folder card or a path button to move them; a folder never moves into itself. | Fixed (new; pm foldersAndDragging; pl foldersAndMoving) |
| 38.7 | New Project asks for the name and the kind: Single file (everything in one file, media copied in; the default) or Linked (media stays where it is); the last choice is remembered. The project is written to the shown folder at once and opened. | Fixed (new; pm newProjectKinds; pl createRenameDuplicate, singleFileProjectsAreCreatedAndRead; sf deleteTheOriginalAndTheClipStaysEditable). sfint: Single file is enabled and the default (QSettings `projectManager/newProjectKind`); a build whose single-file kind cannot create projects still shows it disabled. |

A file that fails to load shows the error and the window stays. | Fixed (new; pm fileMenuAndSwitchingProjects, keysOpenRenameDelete) |

A name that is taken, empty or contains a slash is refused with a message. | Fixed (new; pm keysOpenRenameDelete; pl createRenameDuplicate) |
| 38.10 | Delete (Delete key or the context menu) asks for confirmation and moves the project, or a folder with everything in it, to the system trash (freedesktop trash, restorable from the file manager). | Fixed (new; pl deleteGoesToTheTrash; pm keysOpenRenameDelete) |
| 38.11 | The project open in the editor is marked "Open"; it and the folders containing it cannot be deleted, renamed or moved (a message says why); it can be duplicated and exported. | Fixed (new; pl theOpenProjectIsProtected; pm theOpenProjectCannotBeDeleted) |

| 38.14 | Project Settings in the context menu for the open project (closes the Project Manager and opens the settings dialog). | Decision: other projects would have to be loaded to change their settings, so the entry is disabled for them ("Open the project to change its settings"). |
| 38.15 | A card shows "Media offline (n)" when media files of the project are missing. | Fixed (new; pl missingMediaAndPosterSource; pm missingMediaIsMarked) |
| 38.16 | A broken or unreadable project file (not JSON, not a Kinoklip project, from a newer version, damaged timeline, empty) shows an error card with the reason; the rest of the list is unaffected. | Fixed (new; pl brokenFilesGiveAnErrorAndDoNotStopTheOthers; pm detailsAndBrokenCards) |
| 38.17 | Recent Projects (left side) lists File > Open Recent's projects wherever they are saved; a missing one is marked. | Fixed (new; pm foldersAndDragging) |

Open, Rename (the name inside the file is saved anew), Duplicate and Delete work for both kinds. |

Metadata and posters are cached in the application cache folder (`project-library/` under `$XDG_CACHE_HOME`, keyed by path, modification time and size); nothing is added to the project files (no format change). | Fixed (new; pl metadataIsReadOnAWorkerAndCached, postersAreCachedPerVersion) |
| 38.20 | Hundreds of projects open instantly: with 300 projects the listing takes milliseconds without opening a file, and a cached library shows its cards with every detail at once. | Fixed (new; pl threeHundredProjects: list 6 ms, cold metadata of all 300 51 ms, warm 10 ms; pm threeHundredProjectsInTheWindow: cached show + sort by duration 37 ms) |

Deferred: the rest (docs/archive/plan-v4 lists more than one library and new windows as later). |
| 38.23 | Export converts between the kinds: the save dialog offers both, and the context menu of a linked project has Export as Single File. A linked project exported as `.kinoklip` is packed with every media file it uses, a single-file project exported as `.kkproj` is unpacked (media into `media/...` next to it); with progress and Cancel (a cancelled export leaves nothing), media left linked are listed. | Fixed (new, sfint; pl exportConvertsBetweenKinds) |
| 38.24 | Duplicate and Import copy the project file on a worker thread behind a progress dialog with Cancel (a single-file project holds all its media); the copy goes through "<name>.part" and a cancelled or failed copy leaves nothing behind. Move is a rename and copies nothing. | Fixed (new, sfint; pl largeCopiesReportProgressAndCancelCleanly; pm keysOpenRenameDelete) |
| 38.25 | Duplicate, Delete and Export act on cards selected by clicking (the card view selects single cells; same cause as 33 above). | Fixed (pm keysOpenRenameDelete) |
| 38.26 | Autosave files are named per project: a saved project by its file, a never-saved one by an id of its own, so two untitled projects with the same name (two windows or two running copies) never share one file, and both can be recovered after a crash. A new or opened project in the same window gets a new id. | Fixed (bughunt4: both wrote `Untitled Project-<hash of "unsaved">`, the second overwrote the first; test_bughunt4 spec38_26_untitledProjectsHaveTheirOwnAutosave) |

| 38.29 | Renaming or moving a project, or a folder of projects, in the Project Manager keeps its File > Open Recent and Recent Projects entries. | Fixed (bughunt4: they showed "(missing)"; `ProjectLibrary::moved`; test_bughunt4 spec38_29_recentProjectsFollowRenameAndMove) |
| 38.30 | Save As names a never-saved project after the file it is saved as (as Resolve's Save Project As asks for the name); a project whose name was its file's name follows the new file; a name set in Project Settings stays. | Fixed (bughunt4: the title bar and the autosave kept "Untitled Project" after the first save; test_bughunt4 spec38_30_saveAsNamesTheProject) |
