Use activates that preset, No Thanks keeps Resolve's keys; the bar shows only once (also when ignored) and never again after a choice; Keyboard Customization offers the same presets at any time. | Fixed (new; test_keypresets firstRunChoiceIsShownOnce). Decision: in the Project Manager, the first window a new user sees, rather than an extra dialog; there is no separate Preferences row because the choice is a keyboard profile, stored as `keymap/profile` like Keyboard Customization's. |

## 39. Single-file projects (kkpack branch, v4 part 2)

A project can be one `.kinoklip` file holding the project document, every media file unchanged
and a manifest (an open ZIP layout: stored entries, ZIP64), moved around like any other file.
Media play straight from inside it. Format and crash-safety design: docs/single-file-project.md.
The library (kkpack) and its integration into the editor, the Project Manager, the File menu,
import and the Media Pool (sfint, release 0.4.0) are done.

Tests: ct = tests/pack/test_container.cpp, pp = tests/pack/test_projectpackage.cpp, em =
tests/media/test_embeddedmedia.cpp, ee = tests/export/test_embeddedexport.cpp, sf =
tests/app/test_singlefile.cpp, pl = tests/projects/test_projectlibrary.cpp.

| # | Expected behaviour | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 39.1 | A single-file project is one file with the extension `.kinoklip`: a standard ZIP archive whose first entry is `mimetype` ("application/x-kinoklip"), then the media (`media/<id>/<original file name>`), the manifest (`manifest.json`) and the project document (`project.kkproj`, the same JSON as a linked project). Entries are stored uncompressed; media bytes are the original files. | Fixed (ct roundTrip; pp packAndOpen; sf deleteTheOriginalAndTheClipStaysEditable) |
| 39.2 | Any standard unzip tool lists, tests and extracts the file, and the extracted folder opens in Kinoklip as an ordinary linked project. | Library done (ct standardZipTools, zip64Sparse; pp extractedWithStandardToolOpens) |
| 39.3 | Media inside the file play in place, without unpacking: probing, frame-exact video seeking identical to the same file outside, hardware decoding, audio samples, every still format (Qt, HEIF/AVIF, EXR/DPX via FFmpeg, camera RAW), animated GIF / WebP, thumbnails, waveforms and the render / export path. | Library done (em probeMatches, videoSeekAccuracy, audioSamplesMatch, stillImagesMatch, animationsDecode, thumbnailsAndWaveforms; ee rendersFromInsideTheContainer) |
| 39.4 | Many decoders on many threads read one file at the same time (own file handle each, no shared seek position); a save while they read does not disturb them. | Library done (ct concurrentReaders; em concurrentDecoders, readWhileSaving) |
| 39.5 | Saving appends the new project document and manifest; media are never rewritten, so saving a 50 GB project takes as long as saving a linked one. | Fixed (pp saveDoesNotRewriteMedia; ct roundTrip; sf deleteTheOriginalAndTheClipStaysEditable: File > Save through the container) |
| 39.6 | A crash or power loss at any moment of a save keeps the previously saved state: the file opens with the previous document (also after a torn central directory, damaged newest records, both header slots destroyed, or a media copy cut short). The next save cuts the torn tail off; Repair does it on request. | Library done (ct truncatedSaveKeepsPreviousState, tornRecordsAndDamage, cancelledCopy; pp repairAfterCrash) |
| 39.7 | Files over 4 GB and single media files over 4 GB work (ZIP64). | Library done (ct zip64Sparse: 4 GiB + 120 KiB sparse entry and an entry beyond 4 GiB, read by Kinoklip and Python) |
| 39.8 | Pack (Project Manager > Export as single file, File > Pack Project): a linked project and every media file it uses become one file, with progress and Cancel; offline media and image sequences stay linked and are listed. A cancelled or failed pack leaves no file behind. | Fixed (pp packAndOpen; sf packCompactVerifyUnpackThroughTheFileMenu, saveAsBetweenTheKinds; pl exportConvertsBetweenKinds). File > Pack Project saves first, then offers to open the packed file. Deferred: packing only the used parts of long clips with handles; image sequences inside the file. |
| 39.9 | Unpack: the media are written to a chosen folder (`media/...`, original modification times) with a linked project file that uses them; nothing existing is overwritten. | Fixed (pp unpack; sf packCompactVerifyUnpackThroughTheFileMenu: File > Unpack Project asks for the folder and continues there as the linked project; saveAsBetweenTheKinds: Save As `.kkproj`) |
| 39.10 | Import into a single-file project copies the media into the file with progress and Cancel (a cancelled import leaves the file as it was); a file already stored (same file, or the same content under another name) is stored once. | Fixed (pp addMediaDeduplicates; ct cancelledCopy; sf importCancelAndDuplicateNotice, see 39.19) |
| 39.11 | Compact (File > Compact Project): rewrites the file without media no media pool item uses and without old project versions, shows the size before (and a preview of the size after), writes a temporary file next to it and replaces the original atomically; damaged media make it stop with an error and leave the original untouched. | Fixed (pp compactAndVerify; sf packCompactVerifyUnpackThroughTheFileMenu, see 39.21). Decision: media of clips that are in the Media Pool but on no timeline are kept; remove the clips from the pool first to drop them. |
| 39.12 | Verify checks the index, the project document, the manifest, missing media and the checksum of every stored byte, and reports media the project no longer uses. | Fixed (pp compactAndVerify; sf packCompactVerifyUnpackThroughTheFileMenu, see 39.22) |
| 39.13 | Caches (thumbnails, waveforms, proxies, optimised media, render cache) are not stored in the file; their identity for embedded media is the container, the entry and its checksum. | Library done (em thumbnailsAndWaveforms) |
| 39.14 | The project document format does not change (format 13): embedded media are media pool items with an `embedded` entry name, linked media keep their paths; older builds open an extracted archive. Moving clips between bins or renaming them never touches the media bytes (entry names are stable). | Library done (pp packAndOpen, extractedWithStandardToolOpens) |
| 39.15 | The Media Pool shows whether a clip is stored in the project file or linked. | Fixed (sf mediaPoolShowsWhereEachClipIs; details in 39.23) |
| 39.16 | Privacy: a single-file project does not reveal where its media came from. No absolute path, folder name or home directory is stored in the file (entry names, manifest, document); re-imports are recognised by a key salted per file, and proxy links of embedded media are not stored. Only media the user keeps linked keep their path. | Library done (pp storesNoPaths, addMediaDeduplicates) |
| 39.17 | Opening a `.kinoklip` (File > Open, Open Recent, the Project Manager, the command line) loads the document from inside the file; the window holds the file and its lock until another project is opened. File > Save appends the document; autosaves are written next to the other autosaves, never into the project file, and crash recovery restores a single-file project like a linked one (the restored project saves into its file again). | Fixed (new, sfint; sf deleteTheOriginalAndTheClipStaysEditable, recoveryOfASingleFileProject) |
| 39.18 | Save As converts: saving a linked or never-saved project as `.kinoklip` packs the current state (unsaved changes included) and continues in the new file; saving a single-file project as `.kkproj` unpacks it next to the chosen file and continues linked. The dialog offers both kinds, the project's own (for a new project the New Project kind) first. | Fixed (new, sfint; sf saveAsBetweenTheKinds). Decision: the undo history starts anew after a conversion (the project is opened from the new file). |
| 39.19 | Importing into a single-file project copies the files in first, for every import path: File > Import Media / Subtitle, a drop from a file manager onto the Media Pool or the timeline, the media browser, Relink (the found files) and the Media Pool's Store in Project File (linked clips of the selection). The copy runs on a worker thread behind a progress dialog with Cancel (a cancelled import imports nothing); a file already stored is not copied again and a notice names it. Folders and image sequences stay linked. | Fixed (new, sfint; sf importCancelAndDuplicateNotice) |
| 39.20 | File menu: Pack Project (linked projects), Unpack Project, Compact Project and Verify Project File (single-file projects) are enabled for the kind of the open project. They have no default keys (Resolve has no equivalent commands). | Fixed (new, sfint; sf packCompactVerifyUnpackThroughTheFileMenu; keymap: no keys) |
| 39.21 | Compact Project saves first, then shows the current size, the estimated size after compacting and the unused media, and asks. Playback stops and every reader of the file is released before the rewrite; the file is opened again afterwards (cached thumbnails of its media are dropped) and its clips keep playing. | Fixed (new, sfint; sf packCompactVerifyUnpackThroughTheFileMenu). TODO(pack): on Windows the playback engine's open decoders must be closed too before the rename (POSIX readers keep the old copy). |
| 39.22 | Verify Project File checks every byte with progress and Cancel; a clean file gives a notice, a damaged one lists the problems and offers Repair (cuts off what an interrupted save left, writes a fresh index, checks again). | Fixed (new, sfint; sf packCompactVerifyUnpackThroughTheFileMenu) |
| 39.23 | Media Pool location: in a single-file project each thumbnail shows a small box for media stored in the file or a "Linked" badge for files on disk; the list view has a Location column ("Project file" / "Linked"), the tooltip says it, the metadata panel and Clip Attributes show the location; Reveal in File Manager shows the project file for stored media. A linked project shows no badges. | Fixed (new, sfint; sf mediaPoolShowsWhereEachClipIs) |
| 39.24 | A single-file project open in another window or program, or whose file cannot be written, opens read-only with a notice and "(Read-Only)" in the window title: Save goes to Save As, imports are refused, Compact is disabled. | Fixed (new, sfint; sf lockedOrReadOnlyFilesOpenReadOnly) |
| 39.25 | Media inside a single-file project are never taken for missing files: offline detection (Media Pool, cards, relink, the metadata panel), proxy selection, the render cache keys and subtitle reading go through `media::mediaExists` / `MediaSource`. Owner's case: import a file, save, delete the original; the clip is still there, decodes, plays, can be edited and renders. | Fixed (new, sfint; sf deleteTheOriginalAndTheClipStaysEditable) |

## 40. Edit page effects (fxkeying branch, v4)

The first batches of docs/archive/plan-v4.md part 3: keying (green screen) and a broad set of colour,
blur, light, stylize, distort and transform effects, from general knowledge of how an editor's
effects library behaves. Clean room: our own names, formulas and wording. Tests: `ve` =
`tests/render/test_videoeffects.cpp`, `ve2` = `tests/render/test_videoeffects2.cpp`, `eu` =
`tests/panels/test_effectsui.cpp`.

| # | Expected behaviour | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 40.1 | Effects library > Video Effects shows folders in alphabetical order: Blur, Color, Distort, Key, Light, Stylize, Transform (the v0.1 effects are sorted in too: Gaussian Blur and Sharpen in Blur, Brightness / Contrast and Saturation in Color, Mirror / Flip in Transform, Vignette, Mosaic and Drop Shadow in Stylize). Search looks through every folder and hides folders without a match. The Inspector's Add Effect menu has one submenu per folder. Dragging onto a clip or the Inspector and double-clicking work as for every effect (spec 5.3). | Fixed (eu libraryFolders; ve descriptorsAndDefaults) |
| 40.2 | Chroma Key (Key folder): Screen (Green Screen, Blue Screen, Custom), Key Color, Tolerance, Softness, Clip Black, Clip White, Shrink / Grow, Edge Blur, Spill Removal, Spill Method (Average, Maximum), Invert, View. The screen colour, darker (shadows) and brighter or purer parts of it become transparent; skin tones, greys, black, white, red and blue stay opaque; colours near the screen colour are partly transparent, in a ramp that Tolerance moves and Softness widens. | Fixed (ve chromaKeyMaths, chromaKeyGreenScreen). Decision: the distance is measured in a luma-normalised CbCr plane (BT.709 CbCr divided by Y + 0.1), split into "along the key's direction" (anything at least as saturated as the key counts as screen) and "across it", so shading on the screen keys evenly; plain RGB distance is not used. |
| 40.3 | The Key Color row has an eyedropper button. While it is checked, the timeline viewer shows a crosshair: a click picks the colour under the mouse (3 x 3 frame pixels averaged), a drag averages the dragged rectangle (dashed outline while dragging). The colour is taken from the clip's picture as it enters the key (the key and the effects after it switched off), written as one undo step "Pick Color", and the Screen switches to Custom. Esc cancels; the eyedropper is off while the playhead is outside the clip. | Fixed (eu inspectorPickerButton, overlayPicks, averageColorIgnoresTransparency). Decision: one pick per button press (the button unchecks itself), so the viewer never stays in a mode the user forgot about. |
| 40.4 | Shrink / Grow erodes (negative) or dilates (positive) the matte by up to 20 px, Edge Blur softens it by up to 20 px; both act on the matte only, never smear the colour, and scale with the preview resolution like every pixel parameter. Clip Black / Clip White stretch the matte so values below / above become fully transparent / opaque. | Fixed (ve chromaKeyMatteProcessing, chromaKeyMaths) |
| 40.5 | Spill removal limits the screen channel (green, or blue for a blue screen; Custom: whichever of the two dominates the key colour) to the mean (Average) or the larger (Maximum) of the other two channels, by the Spill Removal amount, on the whole clip. | Fixed (ve chromaKeyMaths, pointEffectsMatchReference chroma_key_blue_max_spill) |
| 40.6 | View: Final Result (the keyed clip over the tracks below), Matte (the alpha as an opaque greyscale picture: white = kept) or Status (opaque: white = solid, black = transparent, grey = partly transparent, to find holes and fringes). The keyers share these views. | Fixed (ve pointEffectsMatchReference chroma_key_matte_view / chroma_key_status_invert / luma_key_matte, chromaKeyStableAcrossBitDepth) |
| 40.7 | The keyed alpha composites over the lower tracks in the viewer, during playback and in the render the same way (premultiplied alpha, the compositor's normal path), and gives the same matte for 8-bit and 10-bit sources of the same picture. | Fixed (ve chromaKeyGreenScreen, chromaKeyStableAcrossBitDepth: max difference 1 / 255, chromaKeyExportEndToEnd) |
| 40.8 | Luma Key: Low, High, Softness, Invert, View. Brightness between Low and High is kept, the rest becomes transparent, with soft ramps of the Softness width outside the range. | Fixed (ve lumaAndHslKeyMaths, pointEffectsMatchReference luma_key) |
| 40.9 | HSL Key: Hue, Hue Width, Hue Softness, Saturation Low / High / Softness, Luminance Low / High / Softness, Invert, View. The selected hue, saturation and luminance range becomes transparent (like the chroma key's colour); greys are never selected while Saturation Low is above 0. | Fixed (ve lumaAndHslKeyMaths, pointEffectsMatchReference hsl_key, hsl_key_inverted) |
| 40.10 | Difference Key (against a clean plate). | Deferred: it needs a new parameter type referencing another clip and a frame, and a second decoded source per rendered frame; not worth a project format change in this batch. |
| 40.11 | Garbage matte: a clip's masks restrict its key. Order on a clip: its effects run top to bottom in the Inspector's list on the clip's upright picture, then the masks multiply the result (spec 27.2), then transform, opacity and compositing. So a mask around the subject removes everything outside it, keyed or not. | Fixed (ve garbageMatteRestrictsTheKey). Decision: masks cannot sit between two effects; they always apply after all effects. |
| 40.12 | Color folder: Exposure (stops, gamma), Temperature / Tint (blue - orange, green - magenta; a grey keeps its brightness), Levels (input black / white, gamma, output black / white on 0 - 255), Invert (amount), Black and White (amount, tint colour, tint amount), Posterize (levels per channel), Color Balance (lift, gamma, gain, each overall and per red / green / blue). All work on the straight colour and leave the alpha alone. | Fixed (ve colorMaths, pointEffectsMatchReference) |
| 40.13 | Blur folder: Box Blur (radius), Directional Blur (length, angle), Radial Blur (spin angle, centre), Zoom Blur (amount, centre). The cost stays bounded for any value (separable or at most 2 x 24 samples per pixel). | Fixed (ve blursSmooth) |
| 40.14 | Light folder: Glow (threshold, radius, intensity, colour): bright parts bleed light, also beyond the clip's alpha. Stylize folder: Film Grain (amount, size, monochrome, animated), Chromatic Aberration (shift at the corners), Edge Detect (strength, amount, invert), Emboss (light angle, height, amount), Stroke (width, colour, outside / inside, opacity: a border around the clip's shape, e.g. titles and keyed or scaled-down clips). | Fixed (ve lightAndStylize, filmGrain) |
| 40.15 | Distort folder: Lens Distortion (positive barrel, negative pincushion, zoom; outside the picture is transparent), Ripple (rings around a centre: amplitude, wavelength, speed in Hz, phase), Wave (straight waves: amplitude, wavelength, direction, speed, phase). Transform folder: Feathered Crop (left / right / top / bottom in percent of the clip, feather inwards from the crop line). | Fixed (ve distortAndCrop) |
| 40.16 | Every numeric parameter of the new effects is keyframable (Inspector diamonds, Keyframes panel, curves); colours, choices and switches are fixed per clip, like the other effects' statics. Effects with no visible change at their values (zero radius, amount, amplitude) are skipped. | Fixed (ve descriptorsAndDefaults, projectRoundTrip, blursSmooth) |
| 40.17 | Project files store the new effects (ids, values, keys, statics) without a format change; files with them open in older versions, which skip the unknown effects with a warning. | Fixed (ve projectRoundTrip). Decision: no new parameter types, so no format bump or migration. |
| 40.18 | The CPU fallback (no OpenGL 3.3) applies the keyers (without Shrink / Grow and Edge Blur) and every Color folder effect with the GPU's formulas; the other new effects are skipped there like before. | Fixed (ve chromaKeyOnCpuFallback, pointEffectsMatchReference cpu rows) |
| 40.19 | Real time: one keyer at 1080p is a single pass (Shrink / Grow and Edge Blur add separable passes on the matte only). | Fixed (ve chromaKeyPerformance prints the timings). Unverified on the owner's Iris Xe: only software GL (llvmpipe) was available to the tests. |
| 40.20 | The other effects of docs/archive/plan-v4 part 3 (Curves, HSL adjust, LUT files, Lens blur, Tilt-Shift, Unsharp Mask, Noise Reduction, Light Rays, Lens Flare, Halation, Bloom, the further stylize, distort and time effects, Corner Pin) and an eyedropper for the HSL Key. | Fixed by the fxbatch2 branch (40.21 - 40.33); what is still open is listed in 40.33. |
| 40.21 | New parameter types. Curve: the Inspector shows a small graph per curve (input left to right, output bottom to top, the diagonal dashed); dragging a point bends the curve in one undo step, a click adds a point (at most 16), a double-click or right-click removes one (at least 2 stay); the curve passes through every point and never overshoots between two (monotone cubic). File: the file name with a Browse button (file dialog with the effect's filter), the full path as tooltip, in italics when the file is missing; Reset clears it. The HSL Key's Hue has an eyedropper like the key colour (spec 40.3): the pick writes the hue of the picked colour (a key at the playhead when Hue is animated). | Fixed (eu hslKeyHuePick, curveRowEdits, fileRowPicks; tests/core/test_effectparams curveParseAndFormat, curveInterpolation). Also fixed: an effect's static parameter changed in the Inspector right after another edit could leave no undo step (the edit wrote into the undo snapshot it shared); eu fileRowPicks undoes it. |
| 40.22 | Color folder, second batch: Curves (master, red, green, blue; the channel curve applies to the master curve's output; Amount keyframable), HSL Adjust (Range: all colours or reds / yellows / greens / cyans / blues / magentas with width and softness, Hue Shift, Saturation, Lightness; greys belong to no range), Vibrance (muted colours gain more than strong ones, skin tones half as much; plus plain saturation), Channel Mixer (each output channel from percentages of the three inputs, Monochrome uses the red row for all). All on the straight colour, alpha unchanged, on the GPU and the CPU fallback alike. | Fixed (ve2 gradeMaths, pointEffectsMatchReference: GPU and CPU within 1 / 255 of the reference) |
| 40.23 | Apply LUT (Color): a .cube file (LUT_1D_SIZE or LUT_3D_SIZE up to 129, DOMAIN_MIN / DOMAIN_MAX, TITLE, comments) applied with trilinear (3D) or linear (1D) interpolation, mixed by Strength. A missing or malformed file leaves the picture unchanged and logs one warning per file; an edited file is picked up within half a second. Files are read through media::MediaSource, so an entry of a single-file project works the same way. | Fixed (ve2 cubeLutParsing, applyLutMatchesReference). Limitation: a single-file project does not pack the LUT (the container's UI wiring is pending); it refers to the file on disk like a linked project. Rendered-frame cache entries made before a LUT file is edited in place are not invalidated. |
| 40.24 | Blur folder, second batch: Unsharp Mask (amount, radius, threshold: differences below it, i.e. noise and flat areas, are left alone), Tilt-Shift (a sharp band at Focus Position with Focus Width, the blur growing over Transition to the full Blur, at an angle), Lens Blur (a disc blur; Bokeh Highlights makes bright spots above the threshold into bright discs), Noise Reduction (an edge-keeping bilateral filter of 1 - 4 px). Cost bounded for any value: Gaussian blurs downscale, the lens blur's 80 taps run on a copy halved until the radius is at most 6 texels, the noise reduction kernel is at most 9 x 9. | Fixed (ve2 blurAndSharpen, lensBlurBokehAndDenoiseEdges) |
| 40.25 | Directional, Radial and Zoom Blur have Repeat Edge Pixels (on by default: the clip's edge pixels continue outwards, as before; off: the layer gets a margin as large as the blur reaches, so the blur spreads beyond a scaled-down, keyed or title clip's shape). Glow already lit the area around a scaled-down clip (padded layer). | Fixed (ve2 pathBlursRepeatEdges) |
| 40.26 | Light folder, second batch: Light Rays (bright parts above the threshold stream away from a centre, length, intensity, colour), Bloom (three layered soft glows of the highlights, intensity, saturation), Halation (a tinted halo, red by default, around bright areas but not on them), Lens Flare (drawn in code, no image assets: a core glow, Streaks rays, a halo ring with a slight rainbow and five ghosts on the line from the light through the frame centre; light position, size, intensity, ghosts, colour). All add light, also over transparent areas of the clip. | Fixed (ve2 lightEffects) |
| 40.27 | Stylize folder, second batch: Pixelate (hexagons, circle dots on a background colour, diamonds; the grid is centred on the clip), Halftone (black dots on white or picture-coloured dots, sized by the brightness, screen angle), Scanlines (spacing, thickness, darkness, angle, roll speed in px/s), Pencil Sketch (line width, strength, colour amount), Abstraction (smoothed flat areas with few tones and dark outlines), Prism (a rainbow split along one direction), Duotone (shadows and highlights colours by brightness). | Fixed (ve2 stylizeEffects; Duotone in pointEffectsMatchReference) |
| 40.28 | Distort folder, second batch: Twirl (angle, radius, centre; strongest at the centre, seamless at the radius), Bulge / Pinch (positive magnifies, negative squeezes, radius, centre), Kaleidoscope (segments, angle, centre; every other copy mirrored), Mirror Split (keep the left, right, top or bottom half or the top-left quarter and mirror it at a movable line), Corner Pin (four corner offsets in pixels, perspective-correct, antialiased edges, the layer grows to fit), Camera Shake (amplitude, frequency, rotation, zoom to hide the edges, motion blur over a half-frame shutter, random seed; the same shake on every play and in the render). | Fixed (ve2 warpEffects, gradeMaths). Decision: Corner Pin is edited with Inspector values only; on-viewer corner handles are left for later so the viewer code (TransformOverlay, worked on by the guides branch) stays untouched. |
| 40.29 | Time folder (new): Echo (1 - 4 earlier frames, Delay frames apart, each weighted by 1 - Decay times the previous one, normalised: a still picture is unchanged, moving parts leave trails), Strobe (the picture is renewed every Hold frames and held in between), Flicker (brightness around 1 +- Amount: a sine at Frequency mixed with smooth noise by Randomness, seed). Echo and Strobe draw the clip (with the effects listed above them) at the other frames; the renderer decodes those frames for the same output frame, so scrubbing, playback and the render agree and nothing depends on the previously shown frame. A chain (Strobe then Echo) reads at most 9 frames. | Fixed (ve2 timeEffects: echo within 3 / 255 of the average of frames 10 and 8; strobe identical to the held frame; timeEffectFrames). The CPU fallback applies Flicker and skips Echo and Strobe. |
| 40.30 | Transform folder: Tile (copies at Tile Size percent, offset, optionally mirrored at the edges so the copies join seamlessly). | Fixed (ve2 warpEffects) |
| 40.31 | Project files store the second batch like the first: ids, values, keyframes, statics; a Curve value is its points as text, a File value is stored as {file: absolute path, relative: path relative to the project file} and found again through the relative path when the project folder moved. | Fixed (ve2 projectRoundTrip; test_effectparams fileParamsEncodeDecode, projectWithLutMovesWithItsFolder). Decision: no format version bump: the structure of the file is unchanged, older versions skip the unknown effects (with their File values) as before (40.17). |
| 40.32 | Cost of the second batch, 1080p frame including readback under software OpenGL (llvmpipe, no GPU): plain solid about 21 ms; pointwise colour, warps, pixelate, halftone, scanlines, flicker, strobe 20 - 70 ms; unsharp mask, lens blur, pencil sketch, prism, camera shake (8 sub-frames), echo 70 - 200 ms; light rays, bloom, halation, tilt-shift, noise reduction, abstraction 200 - 340 ms. Every pass has a fixed number of taps per pixel. | Fixed (ve2 performance prints the timings). Unverified on a hardware GPU (none available to the tests). |
| 40.33 | Still open after the second batch: Corner Pin handles on the viewer; Difference Key (40.10); Freeze Frame (the retime controls already freeze); packing LUT files into single-file projects; further vignette variants (the Stylize folder's Vignette covers the common use). | Deferred |

## 41. Viewer guides (guides branch, v4 part 4)

Guide lines, rulers, safe areas and a grid on the viewers that help placing things but never
appear in a render (docs/archive/plan-v4.md part 4, owner request), from general knowledge of how
editors offer guides (rulers you drag guides out of, safe-area and grid overlays). Clean room:
our own names and wording. Tests: `vg` = `tests/core/test_viewerguides.cpp`, `gu` =
`tests/playback/test_guideoverlay.cpp`, `gr` = `tests/render/test_guidesrender.cpp`.

| # | Expected behaviour | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 41.1 | View > Guides > Add Horizontal Guide / Add Vertical Guide put a guide through the centre of the picture of the active viewer (else the visible timeline viewer). Adding a guide while Show Guides is off turns it on. Each addition is one undo step "Add Guide" and marks the project modified. Clear Guides removes them all ("Clear Guides"). | Fixed (gu addFromTheMenu) |
| 41.2 | View > Show Rulers shows pixel rulers along the top and left edges of both viewers, beside the picture (they never cover it). Units are pixels of the timeline resolution (source viewer: of the clip shown), 0 at the picture's top / left edge; the scale follows Fit and the 50 / 100 / 200 % zoom, so a tick sits beside the pixel it names. Labels are 1, 2 or 5 x 10^n px apart (at least 56 screen px), with minor ticks between; guides are marked on the ruler in their colour. | Fixed (gu rulerMappingUnderZoom; vg rulerSteps) |
| 41.3 | Dragging out of the top ruler adds a horizontal guide, out of the left ruler a vertical one, where the mouse is released; released over a ruler again, nothing is added. | Fixed (gu dragOutMoveAndRemove) |
| 41.4 | Over a guide the cursor becomes a split cursor; dragging moves it in whole pixels with its position shown beside it ("Move Guide"). Dragging it back onto a ruler, or off the picture, removes it ("Remove Guide"; the line turns dashed while it would be removed). Esc during the drag cancels it. Near a guide (4 px) the guide wins over the on-screen transform and mask controls; elsewhere they work as before. | Fixed (gu dragOutMoveAndRemove; vg guideEditsAreUndoable, nearestGuideHitTest) |
| 41.5 | Double-clicking a guide, or its context menu's Edit Guide Position, opens a small dialog with the distance from the top / left edge in px or % of the frame (switching the unit converts the value); OK is one undo step "Set Guide Position". The context menu also has Remove Guide. | Fixed (gu positionDialog) |
| 41.6 | Show Guides hides and shows every guide (hidden guides are neither hit nor snapped to). Lock Guides stops every mouse edit of guides (drag, ruler drag-out, double-click); the menu commands still work. | Fixed (gu lockAndHide). Decision: locked guides still open their context menu, with its entries disabled, so the user sees why nothing happens. |
| 41.7 | Guide Color picks the colour of all guides (default: the colour scheme's focus colour); the rulers mark guides in it. | Fixed (gu safeAreaAndGridActions) |
| 41.8 | View > Guides > Load Guide Preset offers the built-in Centre Cross, Rule of Thirds and Centre and Thirds, then the user's presets; loading replaces the guides ("Load Guide Preset"). Save Guides as Preset stores the current guides under a name (an existing name is replaced) in `guide-presets.json` in the application data folder; Delete Guide Preset removes one. Presets are stored as fractions of the frame, so they fit any resolution. | Fixed (vg presets; gu presetsApplyAndSave) |
| 41.9 | Snap to Guides (on by default): while an on-screen Transform drag moves a clip, its picture's left / centre / right and top / centre / bottom snap onto guides within 6 screen px; moving the anchor point snaps the anchor; scale and crop handles snap the edges they drag. Holding Ctrl during the drag skips snapping. | Fixed (gu transformDragSnapsToGuides). Decision: snapping needs the guides shown; rotation does not snap. |
| 41.10 | The source viewer has guides too, kept per user (one list for every clip, in px of the clip shown), since a media clip has no document to store them in. | Fixed (gu sourceViewerGuides) |
| 41.11 | Timeline guides are saved with the project, per timeline (project format 14: optional timeline `"guides": [{"o": "h" or "v", "pos": px}]`). Format 13 and older projects open without guides and are written back as 14; older builds refuse format 14 files instead of dropping the guides. | Fixed (vg jsonRoundTrip, projectRoundTripPerTimeline, version13FilesMigrate) |
| 41.12 | Guides, rulers, safe areas, aspect markers and the grid never appear in a render, an export, `--render-frame`, the picture of a source drag or the `--screenshot` window grab: they are a layer over the viewer that the renderers never read, and grabs hide it first. | Fixed (gr rendersIgnoreGuides; gu grabsContainNoGuides) |
| 41.13 | View > Safe Area: Action Safe (90 %, solid outline), Title Safe (80 %, dashed), Centre Mark (a small cross), each a toggle; plus one aspect marker (None, 2.39:1, 1.85:1, 4:3, 1:1, 4:5, 9:16) that outlines the largest centred rectangle of that shape and dims the rest of the picture. | Fixed (vg safeAreaAndGridGeometry; gu safeAreaAndGridActions) |
| 41.14 | View > Show Grid draws a grid over the picture; View > Grid Type: Rule of Thirds (default), 4 x 4, or Custom Grid (2 - 32 columns and rows); choosing a type shows the grid. | Fixed (vg safeAreaAndGridGeometry; gu safeAreaAndGridActions) |
| 41.15 | Show / lock / snap, guide colour, rulers, safe areas, aspect marker and grid are the user's settings (the same in every viewer and project, kept across sessions); only the guide positions belong to the project. | Fixed (gu safeAreaAndGridActions). Decision: per user in QSettings (`viewer/overlays/...`), because they are viewing preferences, not part of the edit. |
| 41.16 | None of the new commands has a default key (no known Resolve default); they are assignable in Keyboard Customization. | Fixed (scripts/keymap-resolve-windows.py rows) |
| 41.17 | Snapping of mask points to guides; panning the zoomed viewer (the rulers follow `pictureRect()`, so a pan will move them too); colours per guide. | Deferred |
| 41.18 | Holding Ctrl while dragging a guide (from a ruler or an existing one) snaps it within 8 screen px onto the frame edges, centre, thirds, quarters, action / title safe edges, the shown grid, the aspect marker and the other guides; pressing or releasing Ctrl mid-drag applies at once (owner request on 0.4.0; Ctrl flips snapping as in transform drags, 41.9). | Fixed (gu ctrlDragSnapsTheGuide, vg guideSnapTargets) |
| 41.19 | The mouse wheel over the picture of any viewer (Source, Timeline, Cut page; also over the on-screen controls) zooms in or out by 12.5 % per notch, trackpads in proportion to their delta, keeping the picture point under the cursor in place; from Fit it starts at the fitted size. The range is 10 % - 1600 % (smaller than Fit is allowed). The header's zoom box shows the current percentage ("137%") when it is not one of Fit / 50% / 100% / 200%; choosing an item still works and keeps the point at the centre of the viewer. | Fixed (gu wheelZoomsAboutCursor) |
| 41.20 | Dragging with the middle button pans the zoomed picture; holding Space over the zoomed picture turns the left button into a hand (open / closed hand cursor) that pans too, and a tap of Space there without a drag still plays / stops, on release. A plain left drag keeps doing what it did (Transform / Crop / mask handles, guides, source drag): neither the middle button nor the Space hand ever starts those. The pan stops when only about 48 px of the picture is left in view. At Fit there is nothing to pan. | Fixed (gu middleDragPans). Decision: Space + drag, as in most picture tools, rather than a hand button, because Space alone must keep playing everywhere else; the release-time play applies only while the cursor is over a zoomed picture. |
| 41.21 | Fit to window: Shift+Z (the Zoom to Fit key, `timeline.zoom_fit`) while the focus is in a viewer fits that viewer instead of the timeline; the header's Fit item and a middle-button double-click fit too. Fit always centres the picture again. | Fixed (gu fitResets) |
| 41.22 | Zoomed playback draws only what is visible: the GL surface draws the frame texture as one quad clipped by the viewport, the raster surface scales only the visible part of the frame, and the engine renders at most the timeline resolution however far the viewer is zoomed (wheel steps update the render size coalesced, like a resize). | Fixed (vp zoomedRasterDrawsVisiblePart, zoomModes) |
| 41.23 | Everything drawn over the picture follows zoom and pan through the one picture rectangle (`ViewerSurface::pictureRect()`): guides and their hit test, rulers, safe areas, aspect marker, grid, the Transform / Crop / mask controls, the eyedropper, the multicam grid labels and the overlay text. The two-up trim view keeps its own layout and takes no zoom input while shown. | Fixed (gu middleDragPans, wheelZoomsAboutCursor) |
| 41.24 | Zoom and pan are per viewer and per session: not saved in the project or the settings, and kept when another clip or timeline is shown (as the header zoom was before). | Decision |
| 41.25 | The panning part of 41.17 is done (41.20); snapping of mask points to guides and colours per guide stay deferred. | Fixed (see 41.20) |
| 41.26 | View > Viewer Zoom to Fit (Z, owner request on 0.4.1) resets zoom and pan of the viewer under the pointer, else of the viewer with the focus, else of every visible viewer. | Fixed (gu zKeyFitsTheViewerUnderThePointer) |
| 41.27 | A press, hover or double-click within 12 screen px of a guide takes it (owner request: "if I click near the guide it should still press it"; was 4 px), and the nearest guide wins, so guides close together stay precise. An exact tie goes to the later guide. Within 4 px a guide wins over the on-screen controls; between 4 and 12 px a Transform or Crop handle under the pointer goes first (the plain move area does not count), mask and Dynamic Zoom controls do not (the guide wins). | Fixed (gu pressNearAGuideTakesTheNearest, transformDragSnapsToGuides) |
| 41.28 | A middle click (no drag) on the picture adds a horizontal and a vertical guide crossing at that point, as one undo step, and turns Show Guides on (owner request). Not while guides are locked or outside the picture. A middle drag still pans and a middle double-click still fits (41.20, 41.21), so the guides appear after the double-click time. | Fixed (gu middleClickAddsCrossingGuides) |

## 42. Compositing page (compcore / compui branches, v4)

Kinoklip's node-based compositing page (docs/compositing-page.md): every video clip can get
a node graph whose result replaces its picture. Clean room: behaviour from general
knowledge of node compositors, our own names. Rows of the core (model, evaluation, render)
are in 42a, the page itself (layout, node editor, viewers, inspector) in 42b. Tests: `co` =
`tests/core/test_composition.cpp`, `cr` = `tests/render/test_comprender.cpp`, `ce` =
`tests/export/test_compexport.cpp`.

### 42a Core

| # | Expected behaviour | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 42a.1 | core: A clip holds at most one composition. A new one is MediaIn1 (the clip's own picture) -> MediaOut1 and renders exactly like the clip without a composition (no extra cost). | Fixed (co defaultCompositionPassesThrough; cr defaultCompositionChangesNothing) |
| 42a.2 | core: The node catalogue: MediaIn, MediaOut; Merge (background, foreground, mask; Normal, Screen, Multiply, Add, Overlay, Darken, Lighten, Difference, Subtract; blend, foreground centre / size / angle), Dissolve; Transform (centre, pivot, size, aspect, angle, flips), Crop, Resize, Letterbox; Background (solid, gradient, four corner), Text (font, size, colour, outline, shadow, position), Fast Noise; Rectangle, Ellipse and Polygon masks (soft edge, invert, level, centre, size, angle); Color Corrector (lift / gamma / gain, saturation, levels), Channel Booleans. Every Edit page video effect is also a node ("fx:<id>", one image input and a mask input) with the same parameters, so new effects appear on both pages. | Fixed (co registryHasBuiltins, everyEffectIsANode) |
| 42a.3 | core: Node coordinates follow the node compositor convention a Resolve user knows: positions normalised 0..1 with (0.5, 0.5) the frame centre and y going up, sizes as factors, angles counter-clockwise, mask width / height as fractions of the frame, soft edge as a fraction of the frame width; effect nodes keep their Edit page units. | Fixed (cr mergeForegroundTransform, transformMovesPixels) |
| 42a.4 | core: New nodes get unique names from their type ("Merge1", "Merge2", ...); a rename to an empty or taken name is refused. Node ids are never reused within a composition. | Fixed (co uniqueNames) |
| 42a.5 | core: Adding a node "after" the selected node inserts it between that node and everything it fed, and pushes the downstream nodes right; adding a source (Background, Text, Fast Noise, MediaIn) adds a Merge with the selected node as background and the new node as foreground; adding a mask plugs it into the selected node's free mask input. With MediaOut selected the node goes before it; without a selection it is placed unconnected. | Fixed (co addAfterInsertsIntoChain, addGeneratorAutoMerges) |
| 42a.6 | core: Deleting nodes keeps the chain connected (a reader of a deleted node reads that node's main input); MediaOut cannot be deleted; viewers showing a deleted node are cleared. | Fixed (co removeKeepsChain) |
| 42a.7 | core: Connecting replaces what an input had; a link that would make a loop is refused, and so is a mask output into an image input (an image into a mask input is allowed: its alpha is the matte). A node dropped onto a link is inserted into it when its kinds fit and no loop results. | Fixed (co connectRefusesCyclesAndKinds, insertIntoLink) |
| 42a.8 | core: Copy and paste of a set of nodes keeps the links between them, drops links to nodes outside the set, gives new ids and free names, never copies MediaOut, and works between clips through the clipboard JSON. | Fixed (co copyPasteRemapsIds) |
| 42a.9 | core: Every graph or parameter change is one named undo step (Project::setClipComposition), also for clips of a timeline that is not open; changes during one drag are one step; setting the same value adds no step; Reset Composition removes the composition (undoable). | Fixed (co projectUndo, inactiveTimelineUndo) |
| 42a.10 | core: Compositions are saved with the project (format 15) and survive copy / paste, split (keyframes stay with the content: comp frames are clip-local), duplicate, compound clips and nesting. Format 14 and older projects open with no compositions; damaged graph data is repaired on load. MediaIn nodes reading another media pool item count as uses of that item. | Fixed (co serializationRoundTrip, splitAndTrimKeepComposition, projectFileRoundTripAndMigration, damagedJsonIsRepaired, mediaInCountsAsUse) |
| 42a.11 | core: A clip with a composition shows the composition's output instead of its picture in the Edit page viewer, playback, the Cut page and the render; its Edit page crop, effects, masks, transform, opacity, transitions and compositing apply on top. | Fixed (cr editPageAttributesApplyOnTop; ce compositionInExport) |
| 42a.12 | core: Merge Normal of a half-transparent foreground over a background gives the premultiplied "over" result; the other modes follow the separable blend formulas; Blend scales the foreground. | Fixed (cr mergeHalfTransparentForeground, blendModesMatchCpu) |
| 42a.13 | core: A mask input limits a node's effect to the inside of the mask (an inverted mask to the outside); masks plugged into a mask node combine (union). | Fixed (cr maskedBlurOnlyInside, masksAndLetterbox) |
| 42a.14 | core: A disabled node passes its main input through unchanged; a disabled MediaIn (or a source without input) draws nothing. | Fixed (cr disabledNodePassesThrough) |
| 42a.15 | core: MediaIn shows the owning clip's source at the comp frame with the clip's speed and retime, or another media pool item from its start. | Fixed (cr mediaInOfAnotherItem) |
| 42a.16 | core: The viewers can show any node of a clip's composition at any comp frame (Compositor::renderCompositionNode, zero-copy texture, preview quality), never from the render cache; a missing clip or node shows nothing. | Fixed (cr viewerApiRendersAnyNode, masksAndLetterbox) |
| 42a.17 | core: The render cache key covers the composition's picture-relevant part only (moving nodes or changing the node editor view keeps the cache); a composition counts like an effect for Smart caching. | Fixed (cr cacheKeyFollowsPictureOnly) |
| 42a.18 | core: Without OpenGL the CPU fallback renders every built-in node (masks with hard edges) and the pointwise effect nodes; other effect nodes pass through. Time effects (Echo, Strobe) as nodes show the current frame only. | Fixed (cr GPU / CPU pairs). Decision: documented gaps in src/render/readme.md. |
| 42a.19 | core: 1080p with about ten nodes plays in real time on an integrated GPU (one pass per node, intermediates released early, no readback). | Fixed (cr performance1080p prints the timing) |
| 42a.20 | core: A small composition built like a user builds it (a green-screen MediaIn keyed by a Chroma Key node, moved by an animated Transform, merged over a Background, with Text on top) gives the same picture in the Edit page viewer, in playback (paused and while playing), in the Compositing page viewer (MediaOut) and in the render (ExportJob). | Fixed (ce greenScreenTextTransformEverywhereTheSame) |
| 42a.21 | core: The viewer API decodes MediaIn through the media table and proxies it is given (Compositor::renderCompositionNode media / proxies, nullptr = the compositor's default table), like a timeline render; PlaybackEngine::renderCompositionNode() passes the engine's own table, proxies and quality, and the page's viewers use only that call. | Fixed (cr viewerApiDecodesMediaInThroughItsTable; cp viewerShowsMediaInThroughTheEngine) |

### 42b Page

Leaving the page enables them again. | Fixed (cp timelineCommandsSuspendedOnThePage) |

Picking up a link end works where the port is drawn. | Fixed (ne sideInputsFaceTheirSource; screenshot) |

## 43. In-app help (owner request, 0.4.2)

The app links each part of itself to the matching article of the user guide (`docs/guide/`,
index `docs/guide/index.json`, see src/ui/help/readme.md). Section 42 is the Compositing page
(compui branch). Tests: `tests/app/test_inapphelp.cpp`.

| # | Expected behaviour | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 43.1 | F1 (`help.guide_context`, Help > Help for This…) opens the user guide's article for what the user is on, in the web browser: the contexts of the widget under the pointer, then of the focus widget, then the visible page; the most specific kind with an article wins (effect > menu command > panel > page). Without a match it opens the guide's start page. | Fixed (test_inapphelp f1OnAChromaKeyInTheInspectorOpensTheGreenScreenArticle, f1OnTheDeliverPageOpensTheExportArticle, resolutionOrder). Decision: the pointer before the focus, because in an editor the focus usually stays on the timeline while the user points at what they ask about; an effect anywhere in either chain still beats a panel. |
| 43.2 | Effect context: the entry selected in the Effects library (while the pointer or focus is in the library) and every Inspector section showing an effect, transition, title or generator report `effect:<id>`; F1 with the focus in a Chroma Key section opens "Remove a green screen". | Fixed (test_inapphelp f1OnAChromaKeyInTheInspectorOpensTheGreenScreenArticle, rightClickOffersHowToUse) |
| 43.3 | F1 while a menu is open closes the menus and opens the article of the highlighted command (`action:<id>`), else the usual context. | Fixed (test_inapphelp f1InAnOpenMenuUsesTheHighlightedCommand) |
| 43.4 | Panels report `panel:<key>` (media_pool, effects, edit_index, inspector, keyframes, mixer, metadata, timeline, viewer, render_settings, render_queue) and pages `page:<id>`; on the Deliver page F1 opens the export article. | Fixed (test_inapphelp f1OnTheDeliverPageOpensTheExportArticle) |
| 43.5 | A small "?" (a 16 px question mark in a circle, in the scheme's dim text colour, brighter on hover) sits at the right of a panel's title bar and after the title of an Inspector effect section, only when the guide has an article for that panel or effect; clicking it opens the article. | Fixed (test_inapphelp helpButtonsOnlyWhereAnArticleExists) |

| 43.7 | Help > Guide… opens a small dialog: a search field over the articles' titles, summaries and keywords (every word must match), the list with "Start page" always on top; Enter opens the current entry (the first match while searching), so does a double-click; the dialog then closes. Help > Getting Started opens the getting-started article and is hidden when the guide has none. | Fixed (test_inapphelp guideDialogFiltersAndOpens, gettingStartedFollowsTheIndex, searchLooksAtTitleSummaryAndKeywords) |
| 43.8 | Articles open as `base + file` from the index in the default browser through `platform::openUrl`, so the browser starts with the user's environment, not the AppImage's. | Fixed (test_inapphelp: every test checks the opened URL through the injected opener; platform behaviour: test_childprocessenv) |
| 43.9 | The index is `docs/guide/index.json` bundled as `:/guide/index.json` (resources/guide.qrc), read once. Bad JSON or an unknown `formatVersion` means no "?" buttons and no "How to use" entries, while F1 still opens the start page under the built-in base URL; articles without id / title or with a file that is not a plain relative path are skipped, and a base that is not http(s) falls back to the built-in one. | Fixed (test_inapphelp badIndexesFallBackToTheStartPage, f1WithABrokenIndexOpensTheBuiltInStartPage, helpButtonsOnlyWhereAnArticleExists, bundledIndexIsReadable) |

| 43.11 | The getting-started guide's list of pages describes the Compositing and Color pages that ship, with links to their articles (Help > Getting Started), and its Next steps list the colour article. | Fixed (bughunt9 workflow: the guide still called both pages "not finished yet"; bh9 spec43_11_guideDescribesTheShippedPages) |
| 43.12 | Help > Report a Bug or Inconsistency… (`help.report_bug`, after a separator at the end of the Help menu; Kinoklip's own command, Resolve has none, so no default key) opens one small dialog: choose Bug / Works differently from DaVinci Resolve / Idea; a read-only box shows exactly the text that would be shared; buttons Copy System Info, Open GitHub with This Info (default), Open GitHub Without It, Cancel; one line says Kinoklip sends nothing itself, the browser opens GitHub's form, which the user reviews and submits (GitHub account needed). Cancel opens nothing. | Fixed (test_bugreport helpMenuHasTheEntryWithoutAKey, dialogShowsExactlyWhatIsShared, cancelOpensNothing, copyPutsTheTextOnTheClipboard; keymap script row) |
| 43.13 | What a report can carry (read when the dialog opens, in memory, never stored): Kinoklip version with prerelease (and the configure-time git commit if built from a checkout), package kind (AppImage from `$APPIMAGE` being set, Flatpak from `/.flatpak-info`, else built from source), NAME + VERSION_ID of `/etc/os-release` (the host's, also inside the AppImage), kernel release, `XDG_CURRENT_DESKTOP`, `XDG_SESSION_TYPE` and the Qt platform plugin, Qt runtime (and build) version, FFmpeg release and avcodec / avformat / avutil / swscale / swresample versions, the OpenGL renderer / version / vendor only if the compositor already made a context, the hardware decoding setting plus the backends already tried (none is opened for this), screen count and the primary screen's logical size and scale, widget style, colour theme, system language. Never the user, host or home folder (every value is scrubbed of them), paths, file or project names, IP or serials. | Fixed (test_bugreport collectorHasEveryField, collectorShowsTheKnownOpenGlStrings, collectorSharesNothingPersonal, scrubRemovesWholeWordsOnly, osReleaseIsParsed). Decision: the "Idea" form gets the version only. |

The URL stays at most 7,500 characters: facts are dropped from the end, then the last one is cut with "...", and the box shows the shortened text. "Without it" opens `?template=<form>.yml` only. | Fixed (test_bugreport linkNamesTheTemplateAndPrefillsTheFields, ideaSharesTheVersionOnly, withoutItCarriesNothing, longEnvironmentIsShortenedBelowTheCap, shareOpensThePrefilledForm, withoutOpensTheEmptyForm) |
| 43.15 | The link opens through `guide::openUrl` (platform::openUrl, the user's environment); the app makes no network request of its own, and nothing is sent or stored automatically (docs/privacy.md, the contributor rule in coding-style.md 1b). | Fixed (test_bugreport: every dialog test records the URL through the injected opener) |
| 43.16 | The issue forms in `.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE` are valid, their field ids unique, and they have the ids the app fills (`version`, `environment` except the Idea form); config.yml keeps blank issues and links the user guide. | Fixed (ctest check_issue_forms: scripts/check_issue_forms.py, PyYAML or a simple parser; test_bugreport issueFormsHaveTheFieldsTheAppFills) |
| 43.17 | Opening a link (the bug report's Open GitHub buttons, the guide's "?" buttons, F1, Help > Guide… and Getting Started) and a file or folder (Open Folder in the render notification and the render queue, Reveal in the Media Pool) goes through `platform::openUrl`: first the XDG desktop portal's OpenURI (`OpenURI` for links, `OpenFile` with a descriptor for files and folders), then xdg-open / kde-open / kde-open5 / `gio open` from the user's PATH in that order, then QDesktopServices (not on Wayland with a focus window, where its deferred "true" says nothing); a portal that answers "could not open" (response 2) or fails falls through, a cancelled app chooser does not; a helper that exits non-zero within 1.5 s falls through to the next, one still running then counts as opened and outlives the app. It is synchronous and works when the calling dialog closes right after (1.1.4-beta.1 opened nothing from the bug report dialog on Plasma Wayland: Qt waits for an xdg-activation token and drops the launch when the window is destroyed first). | Fixed (test_openurl defaultOrderIsPortalHelperQt, portalOpensLinks, portalOpensLocalFilesByDescriptor, portalFailureFallsBackToTheHelper, cancelledChooserIsNotRetried, helpersAreTriedInOrder, helperThatFailsTriesTheNext, aSlowHelperCountsAsOpenedAndSurvives) |
| 43.18 | A helper started from the AppImage gets the user's environment: the launcher's `LD_LIBRARY_PATH`, `QT_PLUGIN_PATH`, `XDG_DATA_DIRS`, `QT_QPA_PLATFORMTHEME`, ... are restored or removed and no `KINOKLIP_*` variable is passed; local files are passed as paths; the app's own environment keeps the bundle's values. | Fixed (test_openurl helperGetsTheOriginalEnvironmentAndTheUrl, helperLocalFilesArePaths) |
| 43.19 | When nothing could open a link, a small "Could Not Open the Browser" message shows the link in a selectable read-only field (selected) with Copy Link and Close, over the window that asked; the bug report and Guide… dialogs stay open. Nothing is sent by the app (docs/privacy.md). | Fixed (test_openurl failureShowsTheLinkToCopy; test_bugreport failureKeepsTheDialogAndShowsTheLink) |

## 44. Color page (colorcore branch, owner request 2026-11-04)

Grading engine and a minimal page; design in [color-page.md](color-page.md). Tests:
`tests/core/test_grade.cpp`, `tests/render/test_graderender.cpp`, `tests/render/test_scopes.cpp`,
`tests/export/test_gradeexport.cpp`, `tests/app/test_colorpage.cpp`.

### 44a. Grading core (model, maths, format, rendering, scopes)

| # | Expected behaviour | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 44a.1 | Every clip and every timeline can carry a grade: a node graph of corrector nodes in series, combined by Parallel mixers (equal average) and Layer mixers (each further input laid over the result with its key; the last input on top). An empty or neutral grade renders exactly like no grade. | Fixed (test_grade serialChain, parallelAndLayerMixers, layerFormula; test_graderender bypassAndIdentity) |
| 44a.2 | Primaries: Lift / Gamma / Gain / Offset with R, G, B and master, Contrast about Pivot, Saturation, Hue, Temperature, Tint, Lum Mix and the node's Mix, every one keyframeable. | Fixed (test_grade liftGammaGainReference, primariesReference, keyframedPrimaries) |
| 44a.3 | Curves: Custom Y, R, G, B (monotone, no overshoot), Hue vs Hue, Hue vs Sat, Hue vs Lum (wrapping), Lum vs Sat, Sat vs Sat; a new point on a curve keeps the curve's shape at its neighbours. | Fixed (test_grade customCurves, addCurvePointAnchorsNeighbours, hslCurves, hslRoundTrip) |
| 44a.4 | HSL qualifier with soft edges, per-channel enable and invert limits a node's correction (key = qualifier x windows x Mix). | Fixed (test_grade qualifierKeys) |
| 44a.5 | Power windows Circle, Linear, Polygon and Gradient with inside / outside softness, opacity, invert, position, size and rotation (keyframeable); several windows combine as a union; windows are in the clip's picture coordinates and move, scale and fade with the clip. | Fixed (test_grade windowShapes, windowsShareTheIdSpace; test_graderender windowsMoveWithTheClip) |
| 44a.6 | A corrector can apply a .cube LUT (1D or 3D), tetrahedral by default or trilinear, after its other corrections. | Fixed (test_graderender gpuMatchesCpuReference, cpuBackendMatchesReference) |
| 44a.7 | Pipeline: source (or composition) -> Edit page effects -> clip grade -> masks, crop, transform, opacity -> composite -> timeline grade -> captions. The timeline grade applies over the composite of all tracks. A composition's MediaIn shows the clip ungraded. | Fixed (test_graderender gradeComesAfterEditPageEffects, timelineGradeOverComposite). Decision: Resolve's page order (compositing before colour), docs/color-page.md. |
| 44a.8 | GPU and CPU render the same pixels within 2 levels for every feature (primaries, curves, qualifier, windows, LUTs, mixers); the CPU backend gives the same picture as the GPU backend. | Fixed (test_graderender gpuMatchesCpuReference, cpuBackendMatchesReference, gpuAndCpuBackendsAgree, sharedImageGradesEveryRow) |

| 44a.11 | Keyframed grade values animate over the clip and move with split / trim like other clip keys; a through edit joins only when both halves carry the same grade; the keyframe editors list a "Color NN" group per keyed node. | Fixed (test_graderender keyframedGradeAnimates; test_grade keyframePaths, splitShiftsGradeKeys) |
| 44a.12 | Every grade change is one undo step (dragging a wheel too), on the active or an inactive timeline; an unchanged grade makes no step. | Fixed (test_grade projectUndo, inactiveTimelineUndo; test_colorpage pageKeysAreUndoSteps, primariesAndCurvesEdit) |
| 44a.13 | Project format 16: grades and the gallery are saved; format 15 files load unchanged (nothing graded); damaged grade data is repaired on load. | Fixed (test_grade serializationRoundTrip, damagedJsonIsRepaired, formatVersionAndMigration, projectFileRoundTrip; test_composition) |
| 44a.14 | Gallery: a still keeps a small picture and the grade at grab time, is saved with the project and can be renamed, removed and applied to clips (one undo step for any number of clips). | Fixed (test_grade gallery; test_colorpage galleryAndScopesWithEngine) |

| # | Expected behaviour | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 45.1 | Each render job card has an Edit button (pencil): it loads the job's render settings (and its proxy / optimized media choice) into Render Settings, highlights the card, and the Add to Render Queue button reads Update Job with a Cancel button beside it, as in Resolve. Update Job writes the settings into that job (no new job; a finished job is queued again, a rendering one cannot be edited) with a fresh snapshot of its timeline when that timeline is open; Cancel leaves the job unchanged and brings back the settings shown before; removing the job ends the mode. | Fixed (bughunt4: queued jobs could only be removed and added again; `RenderQueue::updateJob()`, `DeliverPage::editJob()`; bh4 spec45_1_editRenderJob) |
| 45.2 | Export subtitle (File tab, Subtitle Settings) is greyed out for audio-only formats (WAV, MP3, FLAC, ...) as for stills, with a tooltip saying why; a job for such a format never carries subtitles, even when the setting was on before the format changed. | Fixed (bughunt9: the box could be ticked for audio-only formats; `RenderSettingsPanel::updateVisibility()`, `settings()`; bh9 spec45_2_noSubtitleExportForAudio) |

| 45.4 | One mark is enough for In/Out Range, as in Resolve: an Out mark alone renders from the timeline start to Out, an In mark alone from In to the end; Render switches to In/Out Range when either mark is set or moved (also for a second range after a first render) and back to Entire Timeline when both are cleared. | Fixed (owner report on 1.1.0: with only an Out mark Deliver kept Entire Timeline; `DeliverPage::followMarks()` needed both marks; test_deliverpage renderRangeFollowsASingleMark) |
| 45.5 | When the render queue finishes (Render All, or one job started on its own), the desktop shows one system notification, whether or not the window is focused, as HandBrake does: "Render complete" with the job's name or "3 jobs rendered" and the total time ("Timeline 1 rendered in 0:42"); with failures or cancelled jobs "Render finished with errors" and "1 of 3 failed" (one failure adds its reason). Buttons, where the notification server supports them: Open Folder (the file manager on the last rendered file, src/platform/FileManager) and, for a single rendered file, Play (the system's default player). Stopping the render (Stop, or cancelling the job that rendered last) shows nothing. Sent through org.freedesktop.Notifications as "Kinoklip" with the desktop file's icon, or org.freedesktop.portal.Notification inside Flatpak; without a service nothing happens (one line in the kk.notifications log). The taskbar entry is flashed when the window is not active. | Decision (owner request after 1.1.0; Resolve has no such notification; `RenderFinishNotifier`, `platform/DesktopNotifications`; rn = tests/export/test_rendernotify.cpp: summaryTexts, finishedQueueSendsOneNotification, failuresChangeTheText, userCancelSendsNone, freedesktopNotifyCall (Notify signature susssasa{sv}i), portalNotifyCall, noServiceDoesNothing, backendChoice) |
| 45.6 | Preferences > User > UI Settings > Notifications, "Show a system notification when renders finish" (on by default; key `deliver/notifyWhenFinished`) turns 45.5 off: no notification and no taskbar flash. | Fixed (new; rn preferencesCheckbox, preferenceOffSendsNone) |

| 46.1 | Phone clips with a rotation display matrix (90, 270, 180) import upright: the media pool and Clip Attributes give the displayed size (portrait 1080 x 1920 style), and the viewer, thumbnails, the GPU and CPU renderers, the render and the proxy all show the picture the right way up. | Works (bughunt11 check; rw phonePortrait) |
| 46.2 | HDR video (BT.2100 HLG or PQ, BT.2020 primaries: iPhone and Android "HDR video", HDR10) is shown on the SDR BT.709 timeline without being washed out: linearised (HLG with the BT.2100 OOTF), HDR reference white (203 cd/m2) shown near SDR white, BT.2020 converted to BT.709 primaries, highlights rolled off smoothly; SDR video tagged with BT.2020 primaries gets the gamut conversion only. The same maths runs in the GPU video shader and in the CPU converter, so the viewer, thumbnails, renders and proxies agree. There is no HDR output (Resolve's HDR / colour-managed project settings): see 46.6. | Fixed (bughunt11: HLG / PQ were shown as SDR, reference white grey (HLG 189, PQ 146 of 255) and BT.2020 colours dull; `media/HdrToSdr`, `GlVideo`, `FrameConverter`; rw hdrReferenceMaths, hdrIsMappedToSdr) |
| 46.3 | Files that cannot be imported say why, in words a beginner understands: "The file is empty (0 bytes)", "Cannot read the file: permission denied", "The file is incomplete: its index (the MP4 / MOV "moov" atom) is missing, as when a recording or a copy is interrupted", "Not a video, audio or image file, or the file is damaged" (random bytes, a text file named .mp4), "File not found". Nothing crashes or hangs. | Fixed (bughunt11: every case read "Unsupported or damaged file (Invalid data found when processing input)", random bytes named .mp4 included; `openInput()`; rw brokenFileMessages, unreadableFileMessage) |
| 46.4 | An MP4 / MOV whose media data was cut off (a copy or download interrupted, index intact) imports with the length of the media that is really there, and the import tells the user: "The file is incomplete: only the first 3.9 s of 10.0 s are in it". The clip no longer ends in seconds of frozen picture. | Fixed (bughunt11: it imported at its full 10 s and froze after 4 s; `truncatedEndUs()` in MediaProbe, import note in `MediaPool::prepareFile()`; rw incompleteFileIsShortened) |
| 46.5 | Every other real-world file imports with the right duration, frame rate, size and audio format and plays, thumbnails, draws its waveform, renders and makes a proxy: VFR phone H.264 (90 kHz time base), HEVC 10-bit, 4K at 60 fps, ProRes 422 HQ, MJPEG MOV, DNxHR HQ MXF, screen recordings (1366 x 768, 2560 x 1440, 1281 x 721 4:4:4, 5 fps, one 12 s GOP), 5.1 WAV and AAC (downmixed), 24-bit WAV, 44.1 kHz audio (resampled to 48 kHz at the right pitch), mono, MP3 / M4A with cover art (audio only), a 12-megapixel JPEG with EXIF orientation 6, and files with a wrong extension (MP4 named .mov, WAV named .mp3, PNG named .jpg, read by content). An odd frame size renders one pixel smaller in H.264 4:2:0 (which needs even sizes). | Works (bughunt11 check; rw formats; ProRes 4444 alpha, DNxHR SQ, AVI MJPEG, MXF, PNG alpha, WebP, GIF and image sequences: tests/media/test_formats.cpp) |
| 46.6 | Deliberate limits, listed in src/media/readme.md: only one audio stream per file is used (the default one, else the first), a second stream is ignored; the timeline and renders are SDR BT.709 (HDR sources are tone mapped, 46.2; no HDR output, no per-clip input colour space); MP4 / MOV files without their index cannot be opened (46.3); untagged HD clips are read as BT.709. | Decision (bughunt11; rw formats two_audio.mp4) |
| 46.7 | Long file names (200 characters), Cyrillic and emoji in names, spaces in folder names and files in a read-only folder import, play, draw their waveform and make proxies; rendering into a read-only folder fails with "cannot write ...: Permission denied" instead of hanging. | Works (bughunt11 check; rw awkwardPaths) |

## 47. Effects live preview (hoverpreview branch, owner request for 1.1.3)

Tests: `lp` = `tests/panels/test_livepreview.cpp`.

| # | Expected behaviour | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 47.1 | Live Preview, as in Resolve: while the pointer rests on a video effect in the Edit page's Effects library, the timeline viewer shows the frame at the playhead with that effect (default parameters) added to the topmost selected clip under the playhead, else to the topmost video clip there (enabled clips on enabled tracks). With no clip under the playhead nothing changes. | New (`livepreview::buildScene()`, `EffectsLivePreview`; lp effectHoverShowsAndLeaveRestores, effectTargetsTheSelectedClip) |
| 47.2 | The normal picture comes back, pixel for pixel, when the pointer leaves the entry or the list, scrolls, starts a drag, a search or category filter changes, or the panel is hidden; also on playback (no preview while playing), any timeline edit, a timeline switch or a project reset. A playhead move or a selection change while hovering renders the preview again for the new frame / clip. | New (`ViewerPanel::showTemporaryFrame()` / `clearTemporaryFrame()`; lp effectHoverShowsAndLeaveRestores, editsAndPlaybackEndThePreview) |
| 47.3 | A hovered video transition is previewed at the edit nearest the playhead: on the end of the target clip (47.1) nearer to the playhead, the same end a double-click would use; a tail that touches the next clip is that cut. An existing transition there keeps its length and alignment (only the type changes); otherwise one second, centred on a cut, clamped to the clips. The viewer shows a frame of the transition rather than the playhead frame, the playhead does not move. | Decision (owner request "at its midpoint on the nearest edit point"; lp transitionAtTheNearestEdit) |
| 47.4 | A hovered title or generator is laid over the current frame: a clip of the default generator length (Preferences > Editing) with its default look on a new track above all others, the playhead's picture underneath. The Adjustment Clip and audio effects / transitions show nothing. | New (lp titleOverlaysTheFrame, effectTargetsTheSelectedClip) |
| 47.5 | Moving horizontally over a transition or title entry scrubs it, as in Resolve: the pointer's place along the row (left edge = start, middle = midpoint, right edge = last frame) picks the transition frame shown, or how far into the title the playhead sits (so animated titles such as Scroll play through). Effects and generators without animation show the same picture wherever the pointer is. | New (lp transitionAtTheNearestEdit, titleOverlaysTheFrame) |
| 47.6 | The preview never changes the project: it renders a private copy of the timeline; the dirty flag, the undo stack, the timeline data and the playhead stay as they were. | New (lp effectHoverShowsAndLeaveRestores, transitionAtTheNearestEdit, titleOverlaysTheFrame) |
| 47.7 | The Effects library's options menu ("…" at the right of its title bar) has a checkable "Live Preview", on by default and remembered (Preferences key `effects/livePreview`); off, hovering shows nothing, and switching it off ends a preview being shown. | New (Resolve has a Live Preview option; the library's own menu was chosen because the toggle only affects this panel; lp toggleOffShowsNothing) |
| 47.8 | The preview stays responsive: a new entry is rendered only after the pointer rested on it for 100 ms (sweeping over the list renders nothing); moving along the same entry re-renders at once with one render in flight, the newest position following. Renders run on the compositor's render thread at the viewer's size, quality and proxies (`PlaybackEngine::renderAlternative()`); a request made stale by another entry or the end of the hover is cancelled and its picture is never shown. | New (lp staleRequestsAreCancelled) |
| 47.9 | Scope: the Edit page's Effects library only. The Color page has no LUT browser with a hover mechanism (its gallery and LUT choices are untouched); the Compositing page's node library could reuse `EffectsLivePreview` with a composition-node scene, not done. | Decision (owner request) |
| 47.10 | While the pointer scrubs a transition or title entry (47.5), a position line is drawn over its row at the pointer, Resolve-style: a 2 px line in the playhead colour spanning the row's height, the part of the row already passed tinted with it. It follows the pointer and the row (scrolling, resizing) and goes when the preview ends (47.2). Effects and generators, which do not scrub, show none. | New (owner request "a clear line which shows at what point of a transition you are"; lp scrubLineMarksThePosition) |
| 47.11 | Scrubbing is smooth: moves along the same entry are never debounced (only a new entry waits, 47.8); while a render runs only the newest position is remembered and rendered as soon as it finishes (latest wins, never a queue), and a move that maps to the frame already shown renders nothing (an effect, which looks the same along its row, is rendered once). | New (owner request "made smoother"; lp scrubMovesAreCoalesced) |
| 47.12 | The pointer's place maps to frames of the whole transition (or title) frame-accurately: the row's width spans its first to last frame; a frame is left only when the pointer is a quarter frame past the half-way mark to the next one, so jitter around a boundary does not flicker. The preview's scene (the private timeline copy, 47.6) is made once per entry, playhead and selection; scrubbing only moves the frame (transitions) or the title clip's start (titles, in place when no render holds the copy). | New (lp scrubReusesTheScene) |

## 48. Bog fixes: timeline and playback (bogtimeline branch)

A YouTuber's list of Resolve nitpicks, checked on Kinoklip: where Kinoklip already behaves well
the row says so; bugs are fixed; where the complaint is Resolve's deliberate default, Resolve's
behaviour stays the default and a preference offers the alternative.
Tests: `bt` = `tests/timeline/test_bogtimeline.cpp`, `bp` = `tests/playback/test_bogplayback.cpp`,
`kc` = `tests/render/test_keyframedcpu.cpp`, `ep` = `tests/playback/test_editplaying.cpp`.

| # | Expected behaviour | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 48.1 | "The playhead teleports to the mouse": only the ruler moves the playhead; clicks and small drags on clips, gaps and the line through the tracks select and never move it (1.27, 2.6). A press on the playhead's head (its 12 px wide pentagon) grabs it where it is: the playhead does not move at the press and follows the drag by the pointer's motion (to the nearest frame line). A press elsewhere in the ruler jumps the playhead there and scrubs, as in Resolve. Preferences > User > Editing > Timeline ruler: "Pressing the ruler moves the playhead there at once" (on by default, key `edit/rulerPressJumpsPlayhead`); off, a press away from the head only moves the playhead when it is released without moving (a click), and a drag away from the head does nothing. | Fixed bug + new option (a press on the left half of the head lay in the previous frame and stepped the playhead back a frame when zoomed in; `TimelineWidget::pressRuler()`; bt spec48_1_grabbingTheHeadDoesNotJump, spec48_1_rulerPressJumpsByDefault, spec48_1_preferenceMovesOnlyOnAClick, spec48_preferencesShowAndStoreTheOptions) |
| 48.2 | Moving the playhead while playing (ruler click or drag, the viewer's scrub bar, edit and marker navigation keys) carries playback on from the new frame, as in Resolve: the target is shown, then playback resumes exactly there (positions advance frame by frame from the target, no skip forward) with the sound clock in step. Preferences > User > Playback Settings > "Stop playback when the playhead is moved" (off by default, key `playback/stopOnPlayheadMove`): the move pauses on the new frame instead (Premiere). | Already handled (Resolve behaviour since alpha.2: `PlaybackEngine::seekWhilePlaying()`, test_playbackengine seekWhilePlaying; the skip was not reproduced with a fake sound device, not checked on a real one) + new option (`PlaybackBehaviour::stopOnPlayheadMove`; bp spec48_2_*, bt spec48_preferencesShowAndStoreTheOptions) |
| 48.3 | The timeline's vertical scroll position changes only when the user scrolls: clicks (clip, empty track area, ruler), moves, deletes, undo and redo leave every row where it is; when tracks above the visible ones are added, removed or change height (a new top video track, a keyframe lane), the rows on screen stay put. Scrolled to the very top, a new top track comes into view. | Fixed (video tracks stack upwards from the divider, so a track added on top pushed every row down, which looked like the view scrolling up; plain clicks, moves and undo never moved it; `TimelineLayout::scrollAnchor()`; bt spec48_3_verticalScrollStaysWhileEditing) |
| 48.4 | Short transitions are easy to select: a transition drawn narrower than 16 px (zoomed out) is drawn at least 4 px wide and selected from an 8 px band around its centre, which wins over the cut's trim zone under it; the rest of the cut's 7 px trim zone (its outer parts) still selects and trims the edit point. Such a short block has no length handles (zoom in or use the Inspector); wider blocks keep them at their outer edges (13.9). The pointer is an arrow over the selecting part. | Fixed (blocks under 3 px could not be clicked at all, and the 4 px length handles of a narrow block covered it, so a click grabbed an edge; bt spec48_4_shortTransitionIsEasyToSelect, spec48_4_wideTransitionKeepsItsLengthHandles; ts) |
| 48.5 | Clip > Open in Timeline (and the clip menu entry) opens a compound clip / nested timeline with its playhead on the frame the parent's playhead shows in the clip (speed changes included), or on the clip's first visible frame when the playhead is outside it, and scrolls that frame into view. A double-click keeps opening it on the frame under the pointer (26.14). | Fixed (Open in Timeline kept the nested timeline's own playhead, 00:00:00:00 for a new compound clip; `TimelineController::openNestedClip()`; bt spec48_5_openInTimelineMatchesThePlayhead). Decision: Resolve's documentation does not say where the opened timeline is parked; the matching frame is what the double-click already does, so it is the only behaviour, not an option. |
| 48.6 | Keyframes add no waste to playback: evaluating them costs about 4,000 instructions per frame against about 30 million for converting the picture (CPU fallback, callgrind). Playback resolution reduces the output and the picture size asked of the decoder, but an H.264 frame is always decoded whole, so Half and Quarter help the CPU path little; proxies are what makes such media light (30.3, 30.9). Every frame keeps its colours and its swscale context at any picture size: a width that is odd (Quarter of a 1000 x 562 viewer gives 249 x 140) and any source without chroma subsampling (4:4:4, RGB) used to rebuild the context on each frame (1 - 2 ms) and lose the BT.709 matrix and the source's range on every frame after the first. A zoom that animates changes the picture size, so it still rebuilds the context when the size changes, about 10 % of the conversion. Hardware decoding (Preferences > System > Decode Options) and H.264 / H.265 decoding are in the free app. | Fixed bug (`FrameConverter::convert()` passes the flags the context really has; kc spec48_6_oddOutputWidthKeepsTheColours, spec48_6_keyframedClipRendersAtThePlaybackResolution, which prints ms per frame without a limit). Decision: decode sizes stay exact; rounding them up to steps kept the context but measured slower (more pixels to scale at Full and Half), so it was dropped; bicubic stays (bilinear would save about 1 ms per frame and soften the picture). The stutter the presenter saw in Resolve was not reproduced as a keyframe cost here. |
| 48.7 | Editing while the timeline plays (owner request; Edit and Cut pages): every timeline command works during playback (ripple delete, delete, ripple cut, split, trims to the playhead, extend edit, nudge and mouse moves, insert, overwrite, append, the Cut page's smart edits, paste, paste insert, transitions, enable / disable, link, track add and remove, mark in / out, undo, redo). Each edit applies at once and stays one undo step. The playhead keeps its timeline position, also for the commands that move it when paused (4.10, 16.2, 35.12, a ripple delete of a range, a ripple trim of a clip's start): ripple deleting the clip being played goes on with whatever now sits at that position. The picture of the new timeline is on screen within a few frames (frames in flight are cancelled, the frame due is rendered again), the sound within about 43 ms plus the device's own buffer (at most 60 ms): the unheard sound in the ring is mixed again from the new timeline, with short fades and no device restart, and each ring frame stays the frame of the same play position, so A/V sync is untouched. Changes of the arrangement away from what the ring holds need no remix. The end of the timeline follows an edit: playback runs past the old end into appended clips, and when the playhead now lies past the end the usual end of timeline handling stops on the last frame. A loop whose range changes (a ripple delete, new marks) restarts its clock from the current frame, a short stop of the sound. A change of a link, name, colour, flag or a track's lock, height or auto select is no change of pictures and cancels no frame. Dynamic Trim keeps its own playback semantics (8.17, 8.21). | Fixed.
